45!> Allocate the fields of a (flux) forcing type, based on either a set of input
46!! flags for each group of fields, or a pre-allocated reference forcing.
47interface allocate_forcing_type
48 module procedure allocate_forcing_by_group
49 module procedure allocate_forcing_by_ref
50end interface allocate_forcing_type
51
52!> Allocate the fields of a mechanical forcing type, based on either a set of
53!! input flags for each group of fields, or a pre-allocated reference forcing.
54interface allocate_mech_forcing
55 module procedure allocate_mech_forcing_by_group
56 module procedure allocate_mech_forcing_from_ref
57end interface allocate_mech_forcing

62 module procedure myAlloc_3d

68 module procedure find_ustar_mech_forcing
69end interface find_ustar

71! A note on unit descriptions in comments: MOM6 uses units that can be rescaled for dimensional
72! consistency testing. These are noted in comments with units like Z, H, L, and T, along with
73! their mks counterparts with notation like "a velocity [Z T-1 ~> m s-1]". If the units
74! vary with the Boussinesq approximation, the Boussinesq variant is given first.
75
76!> Structure that contains pointers to the boundary forcing used to drive the
77!! liquid ocean simulated by MOM.
78!!
79!! Data in this type is allocated in the module MOM_surface_forcing.F90, of which there
80!! are three: solo, coupled, and ice-shelf. Alternatively, they are allocated in
81!! MESO_surface_forcing.F90, which is a special case of solo_driver/MOM_surface_forcing.F90.
82type, public :: forcing
83
84 ! surface stress components and turbulent velocity scale
85 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
86 omega_w2x => NULL(), & !< the counter-clockwise angle of the wind stress with respect
87 ustar => NULL(), & !< surface friction velocity scale [Z T-1 ~> m s-1].
88 tau_mag => NULL(), & !< Magnitude of the wind stress averaged over tracer cells,
89 !! including any contributions from sub-gridscale variability
90 !! or gustiness, rescaled to units that are more convenient for
91 !! calculating turbulent fluxes and friction velocities [R Z2 T-2 ~> Pa]
92 ustar_gustless => NULL(), & !< surface friction velocity scale without any
93 !! any augmentation for gustiness [Z T-1 ~> m s-1].
94 tau_mag_gustless => NULL() !< Magnitude of the wind stress averaged over tracer cells,
95 !! without any augmentation for sub-gridscale variability
96 !! or gustiness, rescaled to units that are more convenient for
97 !! calculating turbulent fluxes and friction velocities [R Z2 T-2 ~> Pa]
98
99 ! surface buoyancy force, used when temperature is not a state variable
100 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
101 buoy => NULL() !< buoyancy flux [L2 T-3 ~> m2 s-3]
102
103 ! radiative heat fluxes into the ocean [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
104 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
105 sw => NULL(), & !< shortwave [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
106 sw_vis_dir => NULL(), & !< visible, direct shortwave [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
107 sw_vis_dif => NULL(), & !< visible, diffuse shortwave [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
108 sw_nir_dir => NULL(), & !< near-IR, direct shortwave [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
109 sw_nir_dif => NULL(), & !< near-IR, diffuse shortwave [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
110 lw => NULL() !< longwave [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2] (typically negative)
111
112 ! turbulent heat fluxes into the ocean [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
113 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
114 latent => NULL(), & !< latent [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2] (typically < 0)
115 sens => NULL(), & !< sensible [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2] (typically negative)
116 seaice_melt_heat => NULL(), & !< sea ice and snow melt or formation [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2] (typically negative)
117 heat_added => NULL() !< additional heat flux from SST restoring or flux adjustments [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
118
119 ! components of latent heat fluxes used for diagnostic purposes
120 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
121 latent_evap_diag => NULL(), & !< latent [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2] from evaporating liquid water (typically < 0)
122 latent_fprec_diag => NULL(), & !< latent [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2] from melting fprec (typically < 0)
123 latent_frunoff_diag => NULL(), & !< latent [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2] from melting frunoff (calving) (typically < 0)
124 latent_frunoff_glc_diag => NULL() !< latent [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2] from melting glacier frunoff (typically < 0)
125
126 ! water mass fluxes into the ocean [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]; these fluxes impact the ocean mass
127 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
128 evap => NULL(), & !< (-1)*fresh water flux evaporated out of the ocean [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
129 lprec => NULL(), & !< precipitating liquid water into the ocean [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
130 fprec => NULL(), & !< precipitating frozen water into the ocean [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
131 vprec => NULL(), & !< virtual liquid precip associated w/ SSS restoring [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
132 lrunoff => NULL(), & !< liquid river runoff entering ocean [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
133 frunoff => NULL(), & !< frozen river runoff (calving) entering ocean [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
134 lrunoff_glc => NULL(), & !< liquid river glacier runoff entering ocean [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
135 frunoff_glc => NULL(), & !< frozen river glacier runoff entering ocean [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
136 seaice_melt => NULL() !< snow/seaice melt (positive) or formation (negative) [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
137
138 ! carbon content associated with water crossing ocean surface
139 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
140 carbon_content_lrunoff => NULL() !< carbon content associated with liquid runoff [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
141
142 ! Integrated water mass fluxes into the ocean, used for passive tracer sources [H ~> m or kg m-2]
143 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
144 netMassIn => NULL(), & !< Sum of water mass fluxes into the ocean integrated over a
145 !! forcing timestep [H ~> m or kg m-2]
146 netMassOut => NULL() !< Net water mass flux out of the ocean integrated over a forcing timestep,
147 !! with negative values for water leaving the ocean [H ~> m or kg m-2]
148
149 ! heat associated with water crossing ocean surface
150 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
151 heat_content_cond => NULL(), & !< heat content associated with condensating water [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
152 heat_content_evap => NULL(), & !< heat content associated with evaporating water [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
153 heat_content_lprec => NULL(), & !< heat content associated with liquid >0 precip [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
154 heat_content_fprec => NULL(), & !< heat content associated with frozen precip [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
155 heat_content_vprec => NULL(), & !< heat content associated with virtual >0 precip [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
156 heat_content_lrunoff => NULL(), & !< heat content associated with liquid runoff [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
157 heat_content_frunoff => NULL(), & !< heat content associated with frozen runoff [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
158 heat_content_lrunoff_glc => NULL(), & !< heat content associated with liquid runoff [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
159 heat_content_frunoff_glc => NULL(), & !< heat content associated with frozen runoff [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
160 heat_content_massout => NULL(), & !< heat content associated with mass leaving ocean [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
161 heat_content_massin => NULL() !< heat content associated with mass entering ocean [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
162
163 ! salt mass flux (contributes to ocean mass only if non-Bouss )
164 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
165 salt_flux => NULL(), & !< net salt flux into the ocean [R Z T-1 ~> kgSalt m-2 s-1]
166 salt_flux_in => NULL(), & !< salt flux provided to the ocean from coupler [R Z T-1 ~> kgSalt m-2 s-1]
167 salt_flux_added => NULL(), & !< additional salt flux from restoring or flux adjustment before adjustment
168 !! to net zero [R Z T-1 ~> kgSalt m-2 s-1]
169 salt_left_behind => NULL() !< salt left in ocean at the surface from brine rejection
170 !! [R Z T-1 ~> kgSalt m-2 s-1]
171
172 ! applied surface pressure from other component models (e.g., atmos, sea ice, land ice)
173 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: p_surf_full => NULL()
174 !< Pressure at the top ocean interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa].
175 !! if there is sea-ice, then p_surf_flux is at ice-ocean interface
176 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: p_surf => NULL()
177 !< Pressure at the top ocean interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] as used to drive the ocean model.
178 !! If p_surf is limited, p_surf may be smaller than p_surf_full, otherwise they are the same.
179 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: p_surf_SSH => NULL()
180 !< Pressure at the top ocean interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] that is used in corrections to the sea surface
181 !! height field that is passed back to the calling routines.
182 !! p_surf_SSH may point to p_surf or to p_surf_full.
183 logical :: accumulate_p_surf = .false. !< If true, the surface pressure due to the atmosphere
184 !! and various types of ice needs to be accumulated, and the
185 !! surface pressure explicitly reset to zero at the driver level
186 !! when appropriate.
187
188 ! tide related inputs
189 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
190 BBL_tidal_dis => NULL(), & !< Tidal energy dissipation in the bottom boundary layer that can act
191 !! as a source of energy for bottom boundary layer mixing [R Z L2 T-3 ~> W m-2]
192 ustar_tidal => NULL() !< tidal contribution to bottom ustar [Z T-1 ~> m s-1]
193
194 ! iceberg related inputs
195 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
196 ustar_berg => NULL(), & !< iceberg contribution to top ustar [Z T-1 ~> m s-1].
197 area_berg => NULL(), & !< fractional area of ocean surface covered by icebergs [nondim]
198 mass_berg => NULL() !< mass of icebergs [R Z ~> kg m-2]
199
200 ! land ice-shelf related inputs
201 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: ustar_shelf => NULL() !< Friction velocity under ice-shelves [Z T-1 ~> m s-1].
202 !! as computed by the ocean at the previous time step.
203 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: frac_shelf_h => NULL() !< Fractional ice shelf coverage of
204 !! h-cells, from 0 to 1 [nondim]. This is only
205 !! associated if ice shelves are enabled, and are
206 !! exactly 0 away from shelves or on land.
207 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: iceshelf_melt => NULL() !< Ice shelf melt rate (positive)
208 !! or freezing (negative) [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
209 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: shelf_sfc_mass_flux => NULL() !< Ice shelf surface mass flux
210 !! deposition from the atmosphere. [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
211
212 ! Scalars set by surface forcing modules
213 real :: vPrecGlobalAdj = 0. !< adjustment to restoring vprec to zero out global net [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
214 real :: saltFluxGlobalAdj = 0. !< adjustment to restoring salt flux to zero out global
215 !! net [R Z T-1 ~> kgSalt m-2 s-1]
216 real :: netFWGlobalAdj = 0. !< adjustment to net fresh water to zero out global net [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
217 real :: vPrecGlobalScl = 0. !< scaling of restoring vprec to zero out global net ( -1..1 ) [nondim]
218 real :: saltFluxGlobalScl = 0. !< scaling of restoring salt flux to zero out global net ( -1..1 ) [nondim]
219 real :: netFWGlobalScl = 0. !< scaling of net fresh water to zero out global net ( -1..1 ) [nondim]
220
221 logical :: fluxes_used = .true. !< If true, all of the heat, salt, and mass
222 !! fluxes have been applied to the ocean.
223 real :: dt_buoy_accum = -1.0 !< The amount of time over which the buoyancy fluxes
224 !! should be applied [T ~> s]. If negative, this forcing
225 !! type variable has not yet been initialized.
226 logical :: gustless_accum_bug = .true. !< If true, use an incorrect expression in the time
227 !! average of the gustless wind stress.
228 real :: C_p !< heat capacity of seawater [Q C-1 ~> J kg-1 degC-1].
229 !! C_p is is the same value as in thermovar_ptrs_type.
230

232 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
233 ice_fraction => NULL(), & !< fraction of sea ice coverage at h-cells, from 0 to 1 [nondim].
234 u10_sqr => NULL() !< wind magnitude at 10 m squared [L2 T-2 ~> m2 s-2]
235

238 noy_dep => NULL(), & !< NOy Deposition [conc Z T-1 ~> conc m s-1]
239 nhx_dep => NULL(), & !< NHx Deposition [conc Z T-1 ~> conc m s-1]
240 atm_co2 => NULL(), & !< Atmospheric CO2 Concentration [ppm]
241 atm_alt_co2 => NULL(), & !< Alternate atmospheric CO2 Concentration [ppm]
242 dust_flux => NULL(), & !< Flux of dust into the ocean [R Z T-1 ~> kgN m-2 s-1]
243 iron_flux => NULL(), & !< Flux of dust into the ocean [conc Z T-1 ~> conc m s-1]
244 atm_fine_dust_flux => NULL(), & !< Fine dust flux from atmosphere [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
245 atm_coarse_dust_flux => NULL(), & !< Coarse dust flux from atmosphere [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
246 seaice_dust_flux => NULL(), & !< Dust flux from seaice [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
247 atm_bc_flux => NULL(), & !< Black carbon flux from atmosphere [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
248 seaice_bc_flux => NULL() !< Black carbon flux from seaice [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
249
250 real, pointer, dimension(:,:,:) :: &
251 fracr_cat => NULL(), & !< per-category ice fraction [nondim]
252 qsw_cat => NULL() !< per-category shortwave [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
253
254 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
255 lamult => NULL() !< Langmuir enhancement factor [nondim]

257 ! passive tracer surface fluxes
258 type(coupler_2d_bc_type) :: tr_fluxes !< This structure contains arrays of
259 !! of named fields used for passive tracer fluxes.
260 !! All arrays in tr_fluxes use the coupler indexing, which has no halos.
261 !! This is not a convenient convention, but imposed on MOM6 by the coupler.
262
263 ! For internal error tracking
264 integer :: num_msg = 0 !< Number of messages issued about excessive SW penetration
265 integer :: max_msg = 2 !< Maximum number of messages to issue about excessive SW penetration
266
267end type forcing

269!> Structure that contains pointers to the mechanical forcing at the surface
270!! used to drive the liquid ocean simulated by MOM.
271!! Data in this type is allocated in the module MOM_surface_forcing.F90,
272!! of which there are three versions: solo, coupled, and ice-shelf.
273type, public :: mech_forcing
274 ! surface stress components and turbulent velocity scale
275 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
276 taux => NULL(), & !< zonal wind stress [R L Z T-2 ~> Pa]
277 tauy => NULL(), & !< meridional wind stress [R L Z T-2 ~> Pa]
278 tau_mag => NULL(), & !< Magnitude of the wind stress averaged over tracer cells, including any
279 !! contributions from sub-gridscale variability or gustiness [R L Z T-2 ~> Pa]
280 ustar => NULL(), & !< surface friction velocity scale [Z T-1 ~> m s-1].
281 net_mass_src => NULL(), & !< The net mass source to the ocean [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
282 omega_w2x => NULL() !< the counter-clockwise angle of the wind stress with respect
283 !! to the horizontal abscissa (x-coordinate) at tracer points [rad].
284
285 ! applied surface pressure from other component models (e.g., atmos, sea ice, land ice)
286 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: p_surf_full => NULL()
287 !< Pressure at the top ocean interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa].
288 !! if there is sea-ice, then p_surf_flux is at ice-ocean interface
289 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: p_surf => NULL()
290 !< Pressure at the top ocean interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] as used to drive the ocean model.
291 !! If p_surf is limited, p_surf may be smaller than p_surf_full, otherwise they are the same.
292 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: p_surf_SSH => NULL()
293 !< Pressure at the top ocean interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] that is used in corrections
294 !! to the sea surface height field that is passed back to the calling routines.
295 !! p_surf_SSH may point to p_surf or to p_surf_full.
296
297 ! iceberg related inputs
298 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
299 area_berg => NULL(), & !< fractional area of ocean surface covered by icebergs [nondim]
300 mass_berg => NULL() !< mass of icebergs per unit ocean area [R Z ~> kg m-2]
301
302 ! land ice-shelf related inputs
303 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: frac_shelf_u => NULL() !< Fractional ice shelf coverage of u-cells,
304 !! nondimensional from 0 to 1 [nondim]. This is only associated if ice shelves are enabled,
305 !! and is exactly 0 away from shelves or on land.
306 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: frac_shelf_v => NULL() !< Fractional ice shelf coverage of v-cells,
307 !! nondimensional from 0 to 1 [nondim]. This is only associated if ice shelves are enabled,
308 !! and is exactly 0 away from shelves or on land.
309 real, pointer, dimension(:,:) :: &
310 rigidity_ice_u => NULL(), & !< Depth-integrated lateral viscosity of ice shelves or sea ice at
311 !! u-points [L4 Z-1 T-1 ~> m3 s-1]
312 rigidity_ice_v => NULL() !< Depth-integrated lateral viscosity of ice shelves or sea ice at
313 !! v-points [L4 Z-1 T-1 ~> m3 s-1]
314 real :: dt_force_accum = -1.0 !< The amount of time over which the mechanical forcing fluxes
315 !! have been averaged [T ~> s].
316 logical :: net_mass_src_set = .false. !< If true, an estimate of net_mass_src has been provided.
317 logical :: accumulate_p_surf = .false. !< If true, the surface pressure due to the atmosphere
318 !! and various types of ice needs to be accumulated, and the
319 !! surface pressure explicitly reset to zero at the driver level
320 !! when appropriate.
321 logical :: accumulate_rigidity = .false. !< If true, the rigidity due to various types of
322 !! ice needs to be accumulated, and the rigidity explicitly
323 !! reset to zero at the driver level when appropriate.
324 real, pointer, dimension(:) :: &
325 stk_wavenumbers => NULL() !< The central wave number of Stokes bands [rad Z-1 ~> rad m-1]
326 real, pointer, dimension(:,:,:) :: &
327 ustkb => NULL(), & !< Stokes Drift spectrum, zonal [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
328 !! Horizontal - u points
329 !! 3rd dimension - wavenumber
330 vstkb => NULL() !< Stokes Drift spectrum, meridional [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
331 !! Horizontal - v points
332 !! 3rd dimension - wavenumber
333
334 logical :: initialized = .false. !< This indicates whether the appropriate arrays have been initialized.
335end type mech_forcing

340 !>@{ Forcing diagnostic handles
341 ! mass flux diagnostic handles
342 integer :: id_prcme = -1, id_evap = -1

353 integer :: id_total_precip = -1, id_total_vprec = -1
354 integer :: id_total_lprec = -1, id_total_fprec = -1
355 integer :: id_total_lrunoff = -1, id_total_frunoff = -1
356 integer :: id_total_lrunoff_glc = -1, id_total_frunoff_glc = -1
357 integer :: id_total_net_massout = -1, id_total_net_massin = -1
358 integer :: id_total_seaice_melt = -1

360 ! global area averaged mass flux diagnostic handles
361 integer :: id_prcme_ga = -1, id_evap_ga = -1
362 integer :: id_lprec_ga = -1, id_fprec_ga= -1
363 integer :: id_precip_ga = -1, id_vprec_ga= -1
364
365 ! heat flux diagnostic handles
366 integer :: id_net_heat_coupler = -1, id_net_heat_surface = -1
367 integer :: id_sens = -1, id_LwLatSens = -1
368 integer :: id_sw = -1, id_lw = -1
369 integer :: id_sw_vis = -1, id_sw_nir = -1
370 integer :: id_lat_evap = -1, id_lat_frunoff = -1
371 integer :: id_lat_frunoff_glc = -1
372 integer :: id_lat = -1, id_lat_fprec = -1
373 integer :: id_heat_content_lrunoff = -1, id_heat_content_frunoff = -1
374 integer :: id_heat_content_lrunoff_glc= -1, id_heat_content_frunoff_glc= -1
375 integer :: id_heat_content_lprec = -1, id_heat_content_fprec = -1
376 integer :: id_heat_content_cond = -1, id_heat_content_surfwater = -1
377 integer :: id_heat_content_evap = -1
378 integer :: id_heat_content_vprec = -1, id_heat_content_massout = -1
379 integer :: id_heat_added = -1, id_heat_content_massin = -1

382 integer :: id_carbon_content_lrunoff = -1

384 ! global area integrated heat flux diagnostic handles
385 integer :: id_total_net_heat_coupler = -1, id_total_net_heat_surface = -1
386 integer :: id_total_sens = -1, id_total_LwLatSens = -1
387 integer :: id_total_sw = -1, id_total_lw = -1
388 integer :: id_total_lat_evap = -1, id_total_lat_frunoff = -1
389 integer :: id_total_lat_frunoff_glc = -1
390 integer :: id_total_lat = -1, id_total_lat_fprec = -1
391 integer :: id_total_heat_content_lrunoff = -1, id_total_heat_content_frunoff = -1
392 integer :: id_total_heat_content_lrunoff_glc= -1, id_total_heat_content_frunoff_glc=-1
393 integer :: id_total_heat_content_lprec = -1, id_total_heat_content_fprec = -1
394 integer :: id_total_heat_content_cond = -1, id_total_heat_content_surfwater = -1
395 integer :: id_total_heat_content_evap = -1
396 integer :: id_total_heat_content_vprec = -1, id_total_heat_content_massout = -1
397 integer :: id_total_heat_added = -1, id_total_heat_content_massin = -1
398 integer :: id_total_seaice_melt_heat = -1
399
400 ! global area averaged heat flux diagnostic handles
401 integer :: id_net_heat_coupler_ga = -1, id_net_heat_surface_ga = -1
402 integer :: id_sens_ga = -1, id_LwLatSens_ga = -1
403 integer :: id_sw_ga = -1, id_lw_ga = -1
404 integer :: id_lat_ga = -1
405
406 ! salt flux diagnostic handles
407 integer :: id_saltflux = -1
408 integer :: id_saltFluxIn = -1
409 integer :: id_saltFluxAdded = -1
410 integer :: id_saltFluxBehind = -1
411
412 integer :: id_total_saltflux = -1
413 integer :: id_total_saltFluxIn = -1
414 integer :: id_total_saltFluxAdded = -1
415
416 integer :: id_vPrecGlobalAdj = -1
417 integer :: id_vPrecGlobalScl = -1
418 integer :: id_saltFluxGlobalAdj = -1
419 integer :: id_saltFluxGlobalScl = -1
420 integer :: id_netFWGlobalAdj = -1
421 integer :: id_netFWGlobalScl = -1
422
423 ! momentum flux and forcing diagnostic handles
424 integer :: id_taux = -1
425 integer :: id_tauy = -1
426 integer :: id_ustar = -1
427 integer :: id_omega_w2x = -1
428 integer :: id_tau_mag = -1
429 integer :: id_psurf = -1
430 integer :: id_TKE_tidal = -1
431 integer :: id_buoy = -1
432
433 ! tracer surface flux related diagnostics handles
434 integer :: id_ice_fraction = -1
435 integer :: id_u10_sqr = -1
436 integer :: id_atm_fine_dust_flux = -1
437 integer :: id_atm_coarse_dust_flux = -1
438 integer :: id_atm_bc_flux = -1
439 integer :: id_seaice_dust_flux = -1
440 integer :: id_seaice_bc_flux = -1
441
442 ! iceberg diagnostic handles
443 integer :: id_ustar_berg = -1
444 integer :: id_area_berg = -1
445 integer :: id_mass_berg = -1
446
447 ! Iceberg + Ice shelf diagnostic handles
448 integer :: id_ustar_ice_cover = -1
449 integer :: id_frac_ice_cover = -1
450 ! wave forcing diagnostics handles.
451 integer :: id_lamult = -1
452 !>@}
453
454 integer :: id_clock_forcing = -1 !< CPU clock id
455
456end type forcing_diags

458contains
459
460!> This subroutine extracts fluxes from the surface fluxes type. It works on a j-row
461!! for optimization purposes. The 2d (i,j) wrapper is the next subroutine below.
462!! This routine multiplies fluxes by dt, so that the result is an accumulation of fluxes
463!! over a time step.
464720subroutine extractFluxes1d(G, GV, US, fluxes, optics, nsw, j, dt, &
465 FluxRescaleDepth, useRiverHeatContent, useCalvingHeatContent, &
4661440 h, T, netMassInOut, netMassOut, net_heat, net_salt, pen_SW_bnd, tv, &
4672160 aggregate_FW, nonpenSW, netmassInOut_rate, net_Heat_Rate, &
4681440 net_salt_rate, pen_sw_bnd_Rate)
469
470 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< ocean grid structure
471 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< ocean vertical grid structure
472 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
473 type(forcing), intent(inout) :: fluxes !< structure containing pointers to possible
474 !! forcing fields. NULL unused fields.
475 type(optics_type), pointer :: optics !< pointer to optics
476 integer, intent(in) :: nsw !< number of bands of penetrating SW
477 integer, intent(in) :: j !< j-index to work on
478 real, intent(in) :: dt !< The time step for these fluxes [T ~> s]
479 real, intent(in) :: FluxRescaleDepth !< min ocean depth before fluxes
480 !! are scaled away [H ~> m or kg m-2]
481 logical, intent(in) :: useRiverHeatContent !< logical for river heat content
482 logical, intent(in) :: useCalvingHeatContent !< logical for calving heat content
483 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
484 intent(in) :: h !< layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
485 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
486 intent(in) :: T !< layer temperatures [C ~> degC]
487 real, dimension(SZI_(G)), intent(out) :: netMassInOut !< net mass flux (non-Bouss) or volume flux
488 !! (if Bouss) of water in/out of ocean over
489 !! a time step [H ~> m or kg m-2]
490 real, dimension(SZI_(G)), intent(out) :: netMassOut !< net mass flux (non-Bouss) or volume flux
491 !! (if Bouss) of water leaving ocean surface
492 !! over a time step [H ~> m or kg m-2].
493 !! netMassOut < 0 means mass leaves ocean.
494 real, dimension(SZI_(G)), intent(out) :: net_heat !< net heat at the surface accumulated over a
495 !! time step for coupler + restoring.
496 !! Exclude two terms from net_heat:
497 !! (1) downwelling (penetrative) SW,
498 !! (2) evaporation heat content,
499 !! (since do not yet know evap temperature).
500 !! [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2].
501 real, dimension(SZI_(G)), intent(out) :: net_salt !< surface salt flux into the ocean
502 !! accumulated over a time step
503 !! [S H ~> ppt m or ppt kg m-2].
504 real, dimension(max(1,nsw),G%isd:G%ied), intent(out) :: pen_SW_bnd !< penetrating SW flux, split into bands.
505 !! [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2]
506 !! and array size nsw x SZI_(G), where
507 !! nsw=number of SW bands in pen_SW_bnd.
508 !! This heat flux is not part of net_heat.
509 type(thermo_var_ptrs), intent(inout) :: tv !< structure containing pointers to available
510 !! thermodynamic fields. Used to keep
511 !! track of the heat flux associated with net
512 !! mass fluxes into the ocean.
513 logical, intent(in) :: aggregate_FW !< For determining how to aggregate forcing.
514 real, dimension(SZI_(G)), &
515 optional, intent(out) :: nonpenSW !< Non-penetrating SW used in net_heat
516 !! [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2].
517 !! Summed over SW bands when diagnosing nonpenSW.
518 real, dimension(SZI_(G)), &
519 optional, intent(out) :: net_Heat_rate !< Rate of net surface heating
520 !! [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1].
521 real, dimension(SZI_(G)), &
522 optional, intent(out) :: net_salt_rate !< Surface salt flux into the ocean
523 !! [S H T-1 ~> ppt m s-1 or ppt kg m-2 s-1].
524 real, dimension(SZI_(G)), &
525 optional, intent(out) :: netmassInOut_rate !< Rate of net mass flux into the ocean
526 !! [H T-1 ~> m s-1 or kg m-2 s-1].
527 real, dimension(max(1,nsw),G%isd:G%ied), &
528 optional, intent(out) :: pen_sw_bnd_rate !< Rate of penetrative shortwave heating
529 !! [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1].
530
531 ! local
5321440 real :: htot(SZI_(G)) ! total ocean depth [H ~> m or kg m-2]
5331440 real :: Pen_sw_tot(SZI_(G)) ! sum across all bands of Pen_SW [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2].
5341440 real :: pen_sw_tot_rate(SZI_(G)) ! Summed rate of shortwave heating across bands
535 ! [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1]
536 real :: Ih_limit ! inverse depth at which surface fluxes start to be limited
537 ! or 0 for no limiting [H-1 ~> m-1 or m2 kg-1]
538 real :: scale ! scale scales away fluxes if depth < FluxRescaleDepth [nondim]
539 real :: I_Cp ! 1.0 / C_p [C Q-1 ~> kg degC J-1]
540 real :: I_Cp_Hconvert ! Unit conversion factors divided by the heat capacity
541 ! [C H R-1 Z-1 Q-1 ~> degC m3 J-1 or kg degC J-1]
542 logical :: calculate_diags ! Indicate to calculate/update diagnostic arrays
543 logical :: do_enthalpy ! If true (default) enthalpy terms are computed in MOM6
544 character(len=200) :: mesg
545 integer :: is, ie, nz, i, k, n
546
547 logical :: do_NHR, do_NSR, do_NMIOR, do_PSWBR
548
549 !BGR-Jul 5,2017{
550 ! Initializes/sets logicals if 'rates' are requested
551 ! These factors are required for legacy reasons
552 ! and therefore computed only when optional outputs are requested
553720 do_NHR = present(net_heat_rate)
554720 do_NSR = present(net_salt_rate)
555720 do_NMIOR = present(netmassinout_rate)
556720 do_PSWBR = present(pen_sw_bnd_rate)
557 !}BGR
558
559 ! GMM: by default heat content from mass entering and leaving the ocean (enthalpy)
560 ! is diagnosed in this subroutine. When heat_content_evap is associated,
561 ! the enthalpy terms are provided via coupler and, therefore, they do not need
562 ! to be computed again.
563720 do_enthalpy = .true.
564720 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_evap)) do_enthalpy = .false.
565
566720 Ih_limit = 0.0 ; if (FluxRescaleDepth > 0.0) Ih_limit = 1.0 / FluxRescaleDepth
567720 I_Cp = 1.0 / tv%C_p
568720 I_Cp_Hconvert = 1.0 / (GV%H_to_RZ * tv%C_p)
569
570720 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; nz = GV%ke
571
572720 calculate_diags = .true.
573
574 ! error checking
575
576720 if (nsw > 0) then ; if (nsw /= optics_nbands(optics)) call MOM_error(WARNING, &
5770 "mismatch in the number of bands of shortwave radiation in MOM_forcing_type extract_fluxes.")
578 endif
579
580720 if (.not.associated(fluxes%sw)) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
5810 "MOM_forcing_type extractFluxes1d: fluxes%sw is not associated.")
582
583720 if (.not.associated(fluxes%lw)) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
5840 "MOM_forcing_type extractFluxes1d: fluxes%lw is not associated.")
585
586720 if (.not.associated(fluxes%latent)) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
5870 "MOM_forcing_type extractFluxes1d: fluxes%latent is not associated.")
588
589720 if (.not.associated(fluxes%sens)) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
5900 "MOM_forcing_type extractFluxes1d: fluxes%sens is not associated.")
591
592720 if (.not.associated(fluxes%evap)) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
5930 "MOM_forcing_type extractFluxes1d: No evaporation defined.")
594
595720 if (.not.associated(fluxes%vprec)) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
5960 "MOM_forcing_type extractFluxes1d: fluxes%vprec not defined.")
597
598720 if ((.not.associated(fluxes%lprec)) .or. &
599 (.not.associated(fluxes%fprec))) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
6000 "MOM_forcing_type extractFluxes1d: No precipitation defined.")
601
60287120 do i=is,ie ; htot(i) = h(i,1) ; enddo
6036447600 do k=2,nz ; do i=is,ie ; htot(i) = htot(i) + h(i,k) ; enddo ; enddo
604
605720 if (nsw >= 1) then
606720 call extract_optics_slice(optics, j, G, GV, penSW_top=Pen_SW_bnd)
607720 if (do_PSWBR) call extract_optics_slice(optics, j, G, GV, penSW_top=Pen_SW_bnd_rate)
608 endif
609
61087120 do i=is,ie
611
61286400 scale = 1.0 ; if ((Ih_limit > 0.0) .and. (htot(i)*Ih_limit < 1.0)) scale = htot(i)*Ih_limit
613
614 ! Convert the penetrating shortwave forcing to (C * H) and reduce fluxes for shallow depths.
615 ! (H=m for Bouss, H=kg/m2 for non-Bouss)
61686400 Pen_sw_tot(i) = 0.0
61786400 if (nsw >= 1) then
618172800 do n=1,nsw
61986400 Pen_SW_bnd(n,i) = I_Cp_Hconvert*scale*dt * max(0.0, Pen_SW_bnd(n,i))
620172800 Pen_sw_tot(i) = Pen_sw_tot(i) + Pen_SW_bnd(n,i)
621 enddo
622 else
6230 Pen_SW_bnd(1,i) = 0.0
624 endif
625
62686400 if (do_PSWBR) then ! Repeat the above code w/ dt=1s for legacy reasons
62786400 pen_sw_tot_rate(i) = 0.0
62886400 if (nsw >= 1) then
629172800 do n=1,nsw
63086400 Pen_SW_bnd_rate(n,i) = I_Cp_Hconvert*scale * max(0.0, Pen_SW_bnd_rate(n,i))
631172800 pen_sw_tot_rate(i) = pen_sw_tot_rate(i) + pen_sw_bnd_rate(n,i)
632 enddo
633 else
6340 pen_sw_bnd_rate(1,i) = 0.0
635 endif
636 endif
637
638 ! net volume/mass of liquid and solid passing through surface boundary fluxes
639 netMassInOut(i) = dt * (scale * &
640 (((((((( fluxes%lprec(i,j) &
641 + fluxes%fprec(i,j) ) &
642 + fluxes%evap(i,j) ) &
643 + fluxes%lrunoff(i,j) ) &
644 + fluxes%lrunoff_glc(i,j)) &
645 + fluxes%vprec(i,j) ) &
646 + fluxes%seaice_melt(i,j)) &
647 + fluxes%frunoff(i,j) ) &
64886400 + fluxes%frunoff_glc(i,j)))
649
65086400 if (do_NMIOr) then ! Repeat the above code without multiplying by a timestep for legacy reasons
651 netMassInOut_rate(i) = (scale * &
652 (((((((( fluxes%lprec(i,j) &
653 + fluxes%fprec(i,j) ) &
654 + fluxes%evap(i,j) ) &
655 + fluxes%lrunoff(i,j) ) &
656 + fluxes%lrunoff_glc(i,j)) &
657 + fluxes%vprec(i,j) ) &
658 + fluxes%seaice_melt(i,j)) &
659 + fluxes%frunoff(i,j) ) &
66086400 + fluxes%frunoff_glc(i,j)))
661 endif
662
663 ! smg:
664 ! for non-Bouss, we add/remove salt mass to total ocean mass. to conserve
665 ! total salt mass ocean+ice, the sea ice model must lose mass when salt mass
666 ! is added to the ocean, which may still need to be coded. Not that the units
667 ! of netMassInOut are still [Z R ~> kg m-2], so no conversion to H should occur yet.
66886400 if (.not.GV%Boussinesq .and. associated(fluxes%salt_flux)) then
6690 netMassInOut(i) = netMassInOut(i) + dt * (scale * fluxes%salt_flux(i,j))
6700 if (do_NMIOr) netMassInOut_rate(i) = netMassInOut_rate(i) + &
6710 (scale * fluxes%salt_flux(i,j))
672 endif
673
674 ! net volume/mass of water leaving the ocean.
675 ! check that fluxes are < 0, which means mass is indeed leaving.
67686400 netMassOut(i) = 0.0
677
678 ! evap > 0 means condensating water is added into ocean.
679 ! evap < 0 means evaporation of water from the ocean, in
680 ! which case heat_content_massout is computed in MOM_diabatic_driver.F90
68186400 if (fluxes%evap(i,j) < 0.0) netMassOut(i) = netMassOut(i) + fluxes%evap(i,j)
682 ! if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_cond)) fluxes%heat_content_cond(i,j) = 0.0 !??? --AJA
683
684 ! lprec < 0 means sea ice formation taking water from the ocean.
685 ! smg: we should split the ice melt/formation from the lprec
68686400 if (fluxes%lprec(i,j) < 0.0) netMassOut(i) = netMassOut(i) + fluxes%lprec(i,j)
687
688 ! seaice_melt < 0 means sea ice formation taking water from the ocean.
68986400 if (fluxes%seaice_melt(i,j) < 0.0) netMassOut(i) = netMassOut(i) + fluxes%seaice_melt(i,j)
690
691 ! vprec < 0 means virtual evaporation arising from surface salinity restoring,
692 ! in which case heat_content_vprec is computed in MOM_diabatic_driver.F90.
69386400 if (fluxes%vprec(i,j) < 0.0) netMassOut(i) = netMassOut(i) + fluxes%vprec(i,j)
694
69586400 netMassOut(i) = dt * scale * netMassOut(i)
696
697 ! convert to H units (Bouss=meter or non-Bouss=kg/m^2)
69886400 netMassInOut(i) = GV%RZ_to_H * netMassInOut(i)
69986400 if (do_NMIOr) netMassInOut_rate(i) = GV%RZ_to_H * netMassInOut_rate(i)
70086400 netMassOut(i) = GV%RZ_to_H * netMassOut(i)
701
702 ! surface heat fluxes from radiation and turbulent fluxes (K * H)
703 ! (H=m for Bouss, H=kg/m2 for non-Bouss)
704
705 ! CIME provides heat flux from snow&ice melt (seaice_melt_heat), so this is added below
706 ! Note: this term accounts for the enthalpy associated with water flux due to sea ice melting/freezing
70786400 if (associated(fluxes%seaice_melt_heat)) then
708 net_heat(i) = scale * dt * I_Cp_Hconvert * &
709 ( fluxes%sw(i,j) + (((fluxes%lw(i,j) + fluxes%latent(i,j)) + fluxes%sens(i,j)) + &
71086400 fluxes%seaice_melt_heat(i,j)) )
711 !Repeats above code w/ dt=1. for legacy reason
71286400 if (do_NHR) net_heat_rate(i) = scale * I_Cp_Hconvert * &
713 ( fluxes%sw(i,j) + (((fluxes%lw(i,j) + fluxes%latent(i,j)) + fluxes%sens(i,j)) + &
71486400 fluxes%seaice_melt_heat(i,j)))
715 else
716 net_heat(i) = scale * dt * I_Cp_Hconvert * &
7170 ( fluxes%sw(i,j) + ((fluxes%lw(i,j) + fluxes%latent(i,j)) + fluxes%sens(i,j)) )
718 !Repeats above code w/ dt=1. for legacy reason
7190 if (do_NHR) net_heat_rate(i) = scale * I_Cp_Hconvert * &
7200 ( fluxes%sw(i,j) + ((fluxes%lw(i,j) + fluxes%latent(i,j)) + fluxes%sens(i,j)) )
721 endif
722
723 ! Add heat flux from surface damping (restoring) (K * H) or flux adjustments.
72486400 if (associated(fluxes%heat_added)) then
72586400 net_heat(i) = net_heat(i) + (scale * (dt * I_Cp_Hconvert)) * fluxes%heat_added(i,j)
72686400 if (do_NHR) net_heat_rate(i) = net_heat_rate(i) + (scale * I_Cp_Hconvert) * fluxes%heat_added(i,j)
727 endif
728
729 ! Add explicit heat flux for runoff (which is part of the ice-ocean boundary
730 ! flux type). Runoff is otherwise added with a temperature of SST.
73186400 if (useRiverHeatContent) then
732 ! remove lrunoff*SST here, to counteract its addition elsewhere
733 net_heat(i) = (net_heat(i) + (scale*(dt * I_Cp_Hconvert)) * fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff(i,j)) - &
7340 (GV%RZ_to_H * (scale * dt)) * fluxes%lrunoff(i,j) * T(i,1)
735 net_heat(i) = (net_heat(i) + (scale*(dt * I_Cp_Hconvert)) * fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc(i,j)) - &
7360 (GV%RZ_to_H * (scale * dt)) * fluxes%lrunoff_glc(i,j) * T(i,1)
737 !BGR-Jul 5, 2017{
738 !Intentionally neglect the following contribution to rate for legacy reasons.
739 !if (do_NHR) net_heat_rate(i) = (net_heat_rate(i) + (scale*I_Cp_Hconvert) * fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff(i,j)) - &
740 ! (GV%RZ_to_H * (scale)) * fluxes%lrunoff(i,j) * T(i,1)
741 !}BGR
7420 if (calculate_diags .and. associated(tv%TempxPmE)) then
743 tv%TempxPmE(i,j) = tv%TempxPmE(i,j) + (scale * dt) * &
7440 (I_Cp*fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff(i,j) - fluxes%lrunoff(i,j)*T(i,1))
745 tv%TempxPmE(i,j) = tv%TempxPmE(i,j) + (scale * dt) * &
7460 (I_Cp*fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc(i,j) - fluxes%lrunoff_glc(i,j)*T(i,1))
747 endif
748 endif
749
750 ! Add explicit heat flux for calving (which is part of the ice-ocean boundary
751 ! flux type). Calving is otherwise added with a temperature of SST.
75286400 if (useCalvingHeatContent) then
753 ! remove frunoff*SST here, to counteract its addition elsewhere
754 net_heat(i) = net_heat(i) + (scale*(dt * I_Cp_Hconvert)) * fluxes%heat_content_frunoff(i,j) - &
7550 (GV%RZ_to_H * (scale * dt)) * fluxes%frunoff(i,j) * T(i,1)
756 net_heat(i) = net_heat(i) + (scale*(dt * I_Cp_Hconvert)) * fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc(i,j) - &
7570 (GV%RZ_to_H * (scale * dt)) * fluxes%frunoff_glc(i,j) * T(i,1)
758 !BGR-Jul 5, 2017{
759 !Intentionally neglect the following contribution to rate for legacy reasons.
760! if (do_NHR) net_heat_rate(i) = net_heat_rate(i) + (scale*I_Cp_Hconvert) * fluxes%heat_content_frunoff(i,j) - &
761! (GV%RZ_to_H * scale) * fluxes%frunoff(i,j) * T(i,1)
762 !}BGR
7630 if (calculate_diags .and. associated(tv%TempxPmE)) then
764 tv%TempxPmE(i,j) = tv%TempxPmE(i,j) + (scale * dt) * &
7650 (I_Cp*fluxes%heat_content_frunoff(i,j) - fluxes%frunoff(i,j)*T(i,1))
766 tv%TempxPmE(i,j) = tv%TempxPmE(i,j) + (scale * dt) * &
7670 (I_Cp*fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc(i,j) - fluxes%frunoff_glc(i,j)*T(i,1))
768 endif
769 endif
770
771! smg: new code
772 ! add heat from all terms that may add mass to the ocean (K * H).
773 ! if evap, lprec, or vprec < 0, then compute their heat content
774 ! inside MOM_diabatic_driver.F90 and fill in fluxes%heat_content_massout.
775 ! we do so since we do not here know the temperature
776 ! of water leaving the ocean, as it could be leaving from more than
777 ! one layer of the upper ocean in the case of very thin layers.
778 ! When evap, lprec, or vprec > 0, then we know their heat content here
779 ! via settings from inside of the appropriate config_src driver files.
780! if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_lprec)) then
781! net_heat(i) = net_heat(i) + scale * dt * I_Cp_Hconvert * &
782! (fluxes%heat_content_lprec(i,j) + (fluxes%heat_content_fprec(i,j) + &
783! (fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff(i,j) + (fluxes%heat_content_frunoff(i,j) + &
784! (fluxes%heat_content_cond(i,j) + fluxes%heat_content_vprec(i,j))))))
785! endif
786
787 ! When enthalpy terms are provided via coupler, they must be included in net_heat
78886400 if (.not. do_enthalpy) then
789 net_heat(i) = net_heat(i) + (scale * dt * I_Cp_Hconvert * &
790 ((((fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff(i,j) + fluxes%heat_content_frunoff(i,j)) + &
791 (fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc(i,j) + fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc(i,j))) + &
792 (fluxes%heat_content_lprec(i,j) + fluxes%heat_content_fprec(i,j))) + &
7930 (fluxes%heat_content_evap(i,j) + fluxes%heat_content_cond(i,j))))
794 endif
795
79686400 if (fluxes%num_msg < fluxes%max_msg) then
79786400 if (Pen_SW_tot(i) > 1.000001 * I_Cp_Hconvert*scale*dt*fluxes%sw(i,j)) then
7980 fluxes%num_msg = fluxes%num_msg + 1
799 write(mesg,'("Penetrating shortwave of ",1pe17.10, &
800 &" exceeds total shortwave of ",1pe17.10,&
801 &" at ",1pg11.4,",E,",1pg11.4,"N.")') &
8020 US%C_to_degC*Pen_SW_tot(i), US%C_to_degC*I_Cp_Hconvert*scale*dt * fluxes%sw(i,j), &
8030 G%geoLonT(i,j), G%geoLatT(i,j)
8040 call MOM_error(WARNING,mesg)
805 endif
806 endif
807
808 ! remove penetrative portion of the SW that is NOT absorbed within a
809 ! tiny layer at the top of the ocean.
81086400 net_heat(i) = net_heat(i) - Pen_SW_tot(i)
811 !Repeat above code for 'rate' term
81286400 if (do_NHR) net_heat_rate(i) = net_heat_rate(i) - Pen_SW_tot_rate(i)
813
814 ! diagnose non-downwelling SW
81586400 if (present(nonPenSW)) then
81686400 nonPenSW(i) = scale * dt * I_Cp_Hconvert * fluxes%sw(i,j) - Pen_SW_tot(i)
817 endif
818
819 ! Salt fluxes
82086400 net_salt(i) = 0.0
82186400 if (do_NSR) net_salt_rate(i) = 0.0
822 ! Convert salt_flux from kg (salt)/(m^2 * s) to
823 ! Boussinesq: (ppt * m)
824 ! non-Bouss: (g/m^2)
82586400 if (associated(fluxes%salt_flux)) then
8260 net_salt(i) = (scale * dt * (1000.0*US%ppt_to_S * fluxes%salt_flux(i,j))) * GV%RZ_to_H
827 !Repeat above code for 'rate' term
8280 if (do_NSR) net_salt_rate(i) = (scale * 1. * (1000.0*US%ppt_to_S * fluxes%salt_flux(i,j))) * GV%RZ_to_H
829 endif
830
831 ! Diagnostics follow...
83287120 if (calculate_diags .and. do_enthalpy) then
833
834 ! Initialize heat_content_massin that is diagnosed in mixedlayer_convection or
835 ! applyBoundaryFluxes such that the meaning is as the sum of all incoming components.
83686400 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_massin)) then
83786400 if (aggregate_FW) then
83886400 if (netMassInOut(i) > 0.0) then ! net is "in"
8390 fluxes%heat_content_massin(i,j) = -tv%C_p * netMassOut(i) * T(i,1) * GV%H_to_RZ / dt
840 else ! net is "out"
841 fluxes%heat_content_massin(i,j) = tv%C_p * ( netMassInout(i) - netMassOut(i) ) * &
84286400 T(i,1) * GV%H_to_RZ / dt
843 endif
844 else
8450 fluxes%heat_content_massin(i,j) = 0.
846 endif
847 endif
848
849 ! Initialize heat_content_massout that is diagnosed in mixedlayer_convection or
850 ! applyBoundaryFluxes such that the meaning is as the sum of all outgoing components.
85186400 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_massout)) then
85286400 if (aggregate_FW) then
85386400 if (netMassInOut(i) > 0.0) then ! net is "in"
8540 fluxes%heat_content_massout(i,j) = tv%C_p * netMassOut(i) * T(i,1) * GV%H_to_RZ / dt
855 else ! net is "out"
856 fluxes%heat_content_massout(i,j) = -tv%C_p * ( netMassInout(i) - netMassOut(i) ) * &
85786400 T(i,1) * GV%H_to_RZ / dt
858 endif
859 else
8600 fluxes%heat_content_massout(i,j) = 0.0
861 endif
862 endif
863
864 ! smg: we should remove sea ice melt from lprec!!!
865 ! fluxes%lprec > 0 means ocean gains mass via liquid precipitation and/or sea ice melt.
866 ! When atmosphere does not provide heat of this precipitation, the ocean assumes
867 ! it enters the ocean at the SST.
868 ! fluxes%lprec < 0 means ocean loses mass via sea ice formation. As we do not yet know
869 ! the layer at which this mass is removed, we cannot compute it heat content. We must
870 ! wait until MOM_diabatic_driver.F90.
87186400 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_lprec)) then
87286400 if (fluxes%lprec(i,j) > 0.0) then
8730 fluxes%heat_content_lprec(i,j) = tv%C_p*fluxes%lprec(i,j)*T(i,1)
874 else
87586400 fluxes%heat_content_lprec(i,j) = 0.0
876 endif
877 endif
878
879 ! fprec SHOULD enter ocean at 0degC if atmos model does not provide fprec heat content.
880 ! However, we need to adjust netHeat above to reflect the difference between 0decC and SST
881 ! and until we do so fprec is treated like lprec and enters at SST. -AJA
88286400 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_fprec)) then
88386400 if (fluxes%fprec(i,j) > 0.0) then
8840 fluxes%heat_content_fprec(i,j) = tv%C_p*fluxes%fprec(i,j)*T(i,1)
885 else
88686400 fluxes%heat_content_fprec(i,j) = 0.0
887 endif
888 endif
889
890 ! virtual precip associated with salinity restoring
891 ! vprec > 0 means add water to ocean, assumed to be at SST
892 ! vprec < 0 means remove water from ocean; set heat_content_vprec in MOM_diabatic_driver.F90
89386400 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_vprec)) then
89486400 if (fluxes%vprec(i,j) > 0.0) then
8950 fluxes%heat_content_vprec(i,j) = tv%C_p*fluxes%vprec(i,j)*T(i,1)
896 else
89786400 fluxes%heat_content_vprec(i,j) = 0.0
898 endif
899 endif
900
901 ! fluxes%evap < 0 means ocean loses mass due to evaporation.
902 ! Evaporation leaves ocean surface at a temperature that has yet to be determined,
903 ! since we do not know the precise layer that the water evaporates. We therefore
904 ! compute fluxes%heat_content_massout at the relevant point inside MOM_diabatic_driver.F90.
905 ! fluxes%evap > 0 means ocean gains moisture via condensation.
906 ! Condensation is assumed to drop into the ocean at the SST, just like lprec.
90786400 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_cond)) then
90886400 if (fluxes%evap(i,j) > 0.0) then
9090 fluxes%heat_content_cond(i,j) = tv%C_p*fluxes%evap(i,j)*T(i,1)
910 else
91186400 fluxes%heat_content_cond(i,j) = 0.0
912 endif
913 endif
914
915 ! Liquid runoff enters ocean at SST if land model does not provide runoff heat content.
91686400 if (.not. useRiverHeatContent) then
91786400 if (associated(fluxes%lrunoff) .and. associated(fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff)) then
91886400 fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff(i,j) = tv%C_p*fluxes%lrunoff(i,j)*T(i,1)
919 endif
92086400 if (associated(fluxes%lrunoff_glc) .and. associated(fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc)) then
92186400 fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc(i,j) = tv%C_p*fluxes%lrunoff_glc(i,j)*T(i,1)
922 endif
923 endif
924
925 ! Icebergs enter ocean at SST if land model does not provide calving heat content.
92686400 if (.not. useCalvingHeatContent) then
92786400 if (associated(fluxes%frunoff) .and. associated(fluxes%heat_content_frunoff)) then
92886400 fluxes%heat_content_frunoff(i,j) = tv%C_p*fluxes%frunoff(i,j)*T(i,1)
929 endif
93086400 if (associated(fluxes%frunoff_glc) .and. associated(fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc)) then
93186400 fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc(i,j) = tv%C_p*fluxes%frunoff_glc(i,j)*T(i,1)
932 endif
933 endif
934
9350 elseif (.not. do_enthalpy) then
936
937 ! virtual precip associated with salinity restoring. Heat content associated with
938 ! that is *not* provided by the coupler and must be calculated by MOM6.
939 ! vprec > 0 means add water to ocean, assumed to be at SST
940 ! vprec < 0 means remove water from ocean; set heat_content_vprec in MOM_diabatic_driver.F90
9410 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_vprec)) then
9420 if (fluxes%vprec(i,j) > 0.0) then
9430 fluxes%heat_content_vprec(i,j) = fluxes%C_p*fluxes%vprec(i,j)*T(i,1)
944 else
9450 fluxes%heat_content_vprec(i,j) = 0.0
946 endif
947 endif
948
9490 if (associated(tv%TempxPmE)) then
950 tv%TempxPmE(i,j) = (I_Cp*dt*scale) * &
951 ((((fluxes%heat_content_lprec(i,j) + fluxes%heat_content_fprec(i,j)) + &
952 (fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff(i,j) + fluxes%heat_content_frunoff(i,j))) + &
953 (fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc(i,j) + fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc(i,j))) + &
9540 (fluxes%heat_content_evap(i,j) + fluxes%heat_content_cond(i,j)))
955 endif
956
957 endif ! calculate_diags and do_enthalpy
958
959 enddo ! i-loop
960
9613600end subroutine extractFluxes1d
962
963
964!> 2d wrapper for 1d extract fluxes from surface fluxes type.
965!! This subroutine extracts fluxes from the surface fluxes type. It multiplies the
966!! fluxes by dt, so that the result is an accumulation of the fluxes over a time step.
9670subroutine extractFluxes2d(G, GV, US, fluxes, optics, nsw, dt, FluxRescaleDepth, &
9680 useRiverHeatContent, useCalvingHeatContent, h, T, &
9690 netMassInOut, netMassOut, net_heat, Net_salt, Pen_SW_bnd, tv, &
970 aggregate_FW)
971
972 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< ocean grid structure
973 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< ocean vertical grid structure
974 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
975 type(forcing), intent(inout) :: fluxes !< structure containing pointers to forcing.
976 type(optics_type), pointer :: optics !< pointer to optics
977 integer, intent(in) :: nsw !< number of bands of penetrating SW
978 real, intent(in) :: dt !< The time step for these fluxes [T ~> s]
979 real, intent(in) :: FluxRescaleDepth !< min ocean depth before fluxes
980 !! are scaled away [H ~> m or kg m-2]
981 logical, intent(in) :: useRiverHeatContent !< logical for river heat content
982 logical, intent(in) :: useCalvingHeatContent !< logical for calving heat content
983 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
984 intent(in) :: h !< layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
985 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
986 intent(in) :: T !< layer temperatures [C ~> degC]
987 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(out) :: netMassInOut !< net mass flux (non-Bouss) or volume flux
988 !! (if Bouss) of water in/out of ocean over
989 !! a time step [H ~> m or kg m-2]
990 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(out) :: netMassOut !< net mass flux (non-Bouss) or volume flux
991 !! (if Bouss) of water leaving ocean surface
992 !! over a time step [H ~> m or kg m-2].
993 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(out) :: net_heat !< net heat at the surface accumulated over a
994 !! time step associated with coupler + restore.
995 !! Exclude two terms from net_heat:
996 !! (1) downwelling (penetrative) SW,
997 !! (2) evaporation heat content,
998 !! (since do not yet know temperature of evap).
999 !! [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2]
1000 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(out) :: net_salt !< surface salt flux into the ocean accumulated
1001 !! over a time step [S H ~> ppt m or ppt kg m-2]
1002 real, dimension(max(1,nsw),G%isd:G%ied,G%jsd:G%jed), intent(out) :: pen_SW_bnd !< penetrating SW flux, by frequency
1003 !! band [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2] with array
1004 !! size nsw x SZI_(G), where nsw=number of SW bands
1005 !! in pen_SW_bnd. This heat flux is not in net_heat.
1006 type(thermo_var_ptrs), intent(inout) :: tv !< structure containing pointers to available
1007 !! thermodynamic fields. Here it is used to keep
1008 !! track of the heat flux associated with net
1009 !! mass fluxes into the ocean.
1010 logical, intent(in) :: aggregate_FW !< For determining how to aggregate the forcing.
1011
1012 integer :: j
1013 !$OMP parallel do default(shared)
10140 do j=G%jsc, G%jec
1015 call extractFluxes1d(G, GV, US, fluxes, optics, nsw, j, dt, &
1016 FluxRescaleDepth, useRiverHeatContent, useCalvingHeatContent,&
1017 h(:,j,:), T(:,j,:), netMassInOut(:,j), netMassOut(:,j), &
10180 net_heat(:,j), net_salt(:,j), pen_SW_bnd(:,:,j), tv, aggregate_FW)
1019 enddo
1020
10210end subroutine extractFluxes2d
1022
1023
1024!> This routine calculates surface buoyancy flux by adding up the heat, FW & salt fluxes.
1025!! These are actual fluxes, with units of stuff per time. Setting dt=1 in the call to
1026!! extractFluxes routine allows us to get "stuf per time" rather than the time integrated
1027!! fluxes needed in other routines that call extractFluxes.
10280subroutine calculateBuoyancyFlux1d(G, GV, US, fluxes, optics, nsw, h, Temp, Salt, tv, j, &
10290 buoyancyFlux, netHeatMinusSW, netSalt)
1030 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< ocean grid
1031 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< ocean vertical grid structure
1032 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
1033 type(forcing), intent(inout) :: fluxes !< surface fluxes
1034 type(optics_type), pointer :: optics !< penetrating SW optics
1035 integer, intent(in) :: nsw !< The number of frequency bands of
1036 !! penetrating shortwave radiation
1037 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: h !< layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
1038 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: Temp !< prognostic temp [C ~> degC]
1039 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: Salt !< salinity [S ~> ppt]
1040 type(thermo_var_ptrs), intent(inout) :: tv !< thermodynamics type
1041 integer, intent(in) :: j !< j-row to work on
1042 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), intent(out) :: buoyancyFlux !< buoyancy fluxes [L2 T-3 ~> m2 s-3]
1043 real, dimension(SZI_(G)), intent(out) :: netHeatMinusSW !< Surface heat flux excluding shortwave
1044 !! [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1]
1045 real, dimension(SZI_(G)), intent(out) :: netSalt !< surface salt flux
1046 !! [S H T-1 ~> ppt m s-1 or ppt kg m-2 s-1]
1047
1048 ! local variables
10490 real, dimension(SZI_(G)) :: netH ! net FW flux [H T-1 ~> m s-1 or kg m-2 s-1]
10500 real, dimension(SZI_(G)) :: netEvap ! net FW flux leaving ocean via evaporation
1051 ! [H T-1 ~> m s-1 or kg m-2 s-1]
10520 real, dimension(SZI_(G)) :: netHeat ! net temp flux [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1]
10530 real, dimension(SZI_(G), SZK_(GV)) :: dz ! Layer thicknesses in depth units [Z ~> m]
10540 real, dimension(max(nsw,1), SZI_(G)) :: penSWbnd ! penetrating SW radiation by band
1055 ! [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1]
10560 real, dimension(SZI_(G)) :: pressure ! pressure at the surface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
10570 real, dimension(SZI_(G)) :: dRhodT ! density partial derivative wrt temp [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
10580 real, dimension(SZI_(G)) :: dRhodS ! density partial derivative wrt saln [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
10590 real, dimension(SZI_(G)) :: dSpV_dT ! Partial derivative of specific volume with respect
1060 ! to temperature [R-1 C-1 ~> m3 kg-1 degC-1]
10610 real, dimension(SZI_(G)) :: dSpV_dS ! Partial derivative of specific volume with respect
1062 ! to salinity [R-1 S-1 ~> m3 kg-1 ppt-1]
10630 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZK_(GV)+1) :: netPen ! The net penetrating shortwave radiation at each level
1064 ! [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1]
1065
1066 logical :: useRiverHeatContent
1067 logical :: useCalvingHeatContent
1068 real :: GoRho ! The gravitational acceleration divided by mean density times a
1069 ! unit conversion factor [L2 H-1 R-1 T-2 ~> m4 kg-1 s-2 or m7 kg-2 s-2]
1070 real :: g_conv ! The gravitational acceleration times the conversion factors from non-Boussinesq
1071 ! thickness units to mass per units area [R L2 H-1 T-2 ~> kg m-2 s-2 or m s-2]
1072 real :: H_limit_fluxes ! A depth scale that specifies when the ocean is shallow that
1073 ! it is necessary to eliminate fluxes [H ~> m or kg m-2]
1074 integer :: i, k
10750 type(thermo_var_ptrs) :: tv_rate ! local copy for rate-only call to extractFluxes1d
1076
1077 ! smg: what do we do when have heat fluxes from calving and river?
10780 useRiverHeatContent = .False.
10790 useCalvingHeatContent = .False.
1080
10810 H_limit_fluxes = max( GV%Angstrom_H, 1.e-30*GV%m_to_H )
1082
1083 ! Do not update tv%TempxPmE in the diagnostic for the rate-only call to extractFluxes1d
10840 tv_rate = tv
10850 tv_rate%TempxPmE => null()
1086
1087 ! The surface forcing is contained in the fluxes type.
1088 ! We aggregate the thermodynamic forcing for a time step into the following:
1089 ! netH = water added/removed via surface fluxes [H T-1 ~> m s-1 or kg m-2 s-1]
1090 ! netHeat = heat via surface fluxes [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1]
1091 ! netSalt = salt via surface fluxes [S H T-1 ~> ppt m s-1 or gSalt m-2 s-1]
1092 ! Note that unlike other calls to extractFLuxes1d() that return the time-integrated flux
1093 ! this call returns the rate because dt=1 (in arbitrary time units)
1094 call extractFluxes1d(G, GV, US, fluxes, optics, nsw, j, 1.0, &
1095 H_limit_fluxes, useRiverHeatContent, useCalvingHeatContent, &
1096 h(:,j,:), Temp(:,j,:), netH, netEvap, netHeatMinusSW, &
10970 netSalt, penSWbnd, tv_rate, .false.)
1098
1099 ! Sum over bands and attenuate as a function of depth
1100 ! netPen is the netSW as a function of depth
11010 call thickness_to_dz(h, tv, dz, j, G, GV)
1102 call sumSWoverBands(G, GV, US, h(:,j,:), dz, optics_nbands(optics), optics, j, 1.0, &
11030 H_limit_fluxes, .true., penSWbnd, netPen)
1104
1105 ! Adjust netSalt to reflect dilution effect of FW flux
1106 ! [S H T-1 ~> ppt m s-1 or ppt kg m-2 s-1]
11070 netSalt(G%isc:G%iec) = netSalt(G%isc:G%iec) - Salt(G%isc:G%iec,j,1) * netH(G%isc:G%iec)
1108
1109 ! Add in the SW heating for purposes of calculating the net
1110 ! surface buoyancy flux affecting the top layer.
1111 ! [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1]
1112 !netHeat(:) = netHeatMinusSW(:) + sum( penSWbnd, dim=1 )
11130 netHeat(G%isc:G%iec) = netHeatMinusSW(G%isc:G%iec) + netPen(G%isc:G%iec,1)
1114
1115 ! Determine the buoyancy flux
11160 pressure(:) = 0.
11170 if (associated(tv%p_surf)) then ; do i=G%isc,G%iec ; pressure(i) = tv%p_surf(i,j) ; enddo ; endif
1118
11190 if ((.not.GV%Boussinesq) .and. (.not.GV%semi_Boussinesq)) then
11200 g_conv = GV%g_Earth * GV%H_to_RZ
1121
1122 ! Specific volume derivatives
1123 call calculate_specific_vol_derivs(Temp(:,j,1), Salt(:,j,1), pressure, dSpV_dT, dSpV_dS, &
11240 tv%eqn_of_state, EOS_domain(G%HI))
1125
1126 ! Convert to a buoyancy flux [L2 T-3 ~> m2 s-3], first excluding penetrating SW heating
11270 do i=G%isc,G%iec
11280 buoyancyFlux(i,1) = g_conv * (dSpV_dS(i) * netSalt(i) + dSpV_dT(i) * netHeat(i))
1129 enddo
1130 ! We also have a penetrative buoyancy flux associated with penetrative SW
11310 do k=2,GV%ke+1 ; do i=G%isc,G%iec
11320 buoyancyFlux(i,k) = g_conv * ( dSpV_dT(i) * netPen(i,k) ) ! [L2 T-3 ~> m2 s-3]
1133 enddo ; enddo
1134 else
11350 GoRho = (GV%g_Earth * GV%H_to_Z) / GV%Rho0
1136
1137 ! Density derivatives
1138 call calculate_density_derivs(Temp(:,j,1), Salt(:,j,1), pressure, dRhodT, dRhodS, &
11390 tv%eqn_of_state, EOS_domain(G%HI))
1140
1141 ! Convert to a buoyancy flux [L2 T-3 ~> m2 s-3], excluding penetrating SW heating
11420 do i=G%isc,G%iec
11430 buoyancyFlux(i,1) = - GoRho * ( dRhodS(i) * netSalt(i) + dRhodT(i) * netHeat(i) )
1144 enddo
1145 ! We also have a penetrative buoyancy flux associated with penetrative SW
11460 do k=2,GV%ke+1 ; do i=G%isc,G%iec
11470 buoyancyFlux(i,k) = - GoRho * ( dRhodT(i) * netPen(i,k) ) ! [L2 T-3 ~> m2 s-3]
1148 enddo ; enddo
1149 endif
1150
11510end subroutine calculateBuoyancyFlux1d
1152
1153
1154!> Calculates surface buoyancy flux by adding up the heat, FW and salt fluxes,
1155!! for 2d arrays. This is a wrapper for calculateBuoyancyFlux1d.
11560subroutine calculateBuoyancyFlux2d(G, GV, US, fluxes, optics, h, Temp, Salt, tv, &
11570 buoyancyFlux, netHeatMinusSW, netSalt)
1158 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< ocean grid
1159 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< ocean vertical grid structure
1160 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
1161 type(forcing), intent(inout) :: fluxes !< surface fluxes
1162 type(optics_type), pointer :: optics !< SW ocean optics
1163 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: h !< layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
1164 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: Temp !< temperature [C ~> degC]
1165 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: Salt !< salinity [S ~> ppt]
1166 type(thermo_var_ptrs), intent(inout) :: tv !< thermodynamics type
1167 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), intent(inout) :: buoyancyFlux !< buoyancy fluxes [L2 T-3 ~> m2 s-3]
1168 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(inout) :: netHeatMinusSW !< surface heat flux excluding shortwave
1169 !! [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1]
1170 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(inout) :: netSalt !< Net surface salt flux
1171 !! [S H T-1 ~> ppt m s-1 or ppt kg m-2 s-1]
1172
1173 ! local variables
1174 integer :: j
1175
1176 !$OMP parallel do default(shared)
11770 do j=G%jsc,G%jec
1178 call calculateBuoyancyFlux1d(G, GV, US, fluxes, optics, optics_nbands(optics), h, Temp, Salt, &
11790 tv, j, buoyancyFlux(:,j,:), netHeatMinusSW(:,j), netSalt(:,j))
1180 enddo
1181
11820end subroutine calculateBuoyancyFlux2d

1184
1185!> Determine the friction velocity from the contenxts of a forcing type, perhaps
1186!! using the evolving surface density.
11871subroutine find_ustar_fluxes(fluxes, tv, U_star, G, GV, US, halo, H_T_units)
1188 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure
1189 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< The ocean's vertical grid structure
1190 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
1191 type(forcing), intent(in) :: fluxes !< Surface fluxes container
1192 type(thermo_var_ptrs), intent(in) :: tv !< Structure containing pointers to any
1193 !! available thermodynamic fields.
1194 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), &
1195 intent(out) :: U_star !< The surface friction velocity [Z T-1 ~> m s-1] or
1196 !! [H T-1 ~> m s-1 or kg m-2 s-1], depending on H_T_units.
1197 integer, optional, intent(in) :: halo !< The extra halo size to fill in, 0 by default
1198 logical, optional, intent(in) :: H_T_units !< If present and true, return U_star in units
1199 !! of [H T-1 ~> m s-1 or kg m-2 s-1]
1200
1201 ! Local variables
1202 real :: I_rho ! The inverse of the reference density [R-1 ~> m3 kg-1]
1203 ! or in some semi-Boussinesq cases the reference
1204 ! density [H2 Z-2 R-1 ~> m3 kg-1 or kg m-3]
1205 logical :: Z_T_units ! If true, U_star is returned in units of [Z T-1 ~> m s-1], otherwise it is
1206 ! returned in [H T-1 ~> m s-1 or kg m-2 s-1]
1207 integer :: i, j, is, ie, js, je, hs
1208
12091 hs = 0 ; if (present(halo)) hs = max(halo, 0)
12101 is = G%isc - hs ; ie = G%iec + hs ; js = G%jsc - hs ; je = G%jec + hs
1211
12121 Z_T_units = .true. ; if (present(H_T_units)) Z_T_units = .not.H_T_units
1213
12141 if (.not.(associated(fluxes%ustar) .or. associated(fluxes%tau_mag))) &
12150 call MOM_error(FATAL, "find_ustar_fluxes requires that either ustar or tau_mag be associated.")
1216
12171 if (associated(fluxes%ustar) .and. (GV%Boussinesq .or. .not.associated(fluxes%tau_mag))) then
12181 if (Z_T_units) then
121964981 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
122064980 U_star(i,j) = fluxes%ustar(i,j)
1221 enddo ; enddo
1222 else
12230 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
12240 U_star(i,j) = GV%Z_to_H * fluxes%ustar(i,j)
1225 enddo ; enddo
1226 endif
12270 elseif (allocated(tv%SpV_avg)) then
12280 if (tv%valid_SpV_halo < 0) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
12290 "find_ustar_fluxes called in non-Boussinesq mode with invalid values of SpV_avg.")
12300 if (tv%valid_SpV_halo < hs) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
12310 "find_ustar_fluxes called in non-Boussinesq mode with insufficient valid values of SpV_avg.")
12320 if (Z_T_units) then
12330 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
12340 U_star(i,j) = sqrt(fluxes%tau_mag(i,j) * tv%SpV_avg(i,j,1))
1235 enddo ; enddo
1236 else
12370 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
12380 U_star(i,j) = GV%RZ_to_H * sqrt(fluxes%tau_mag(i,j) / tv%SpV_avg(i,j,1))
1239 enddo ; enddo
1240 endif
1241 else
12420 I_rho = GV%Z_to_H * GV%RZ_to_H
12430 if (Z_T_units) I_rho = GV%H_to_Z * GV%RZ_to_H ! == 1.0 / GV%Rho0
12440 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
12450 U_star(i,j) = sqrt(fluxes%tau_mag(i,j) * I_rho)
1246 enddo ; enddo
1247 endif
1248
1249 !$omp target update to(U_star)
1250
12511end subroutine find_ustar_fluxes

1254!> Determine the friction velocity from the contenxts of a forcing type, perhaps
1255!! using the evolving surface density.
12562160subroutine find_ustar_mech_forcing(forces, tv, U_star, G, GV, US, halo, H_T_units)
1257 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure
1258 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< The ocean's vertical grid structure
1259 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
1260 type(mech_forcing), intent(in) :: forces !< Surface forces container
1261 type(thermo_var_ptrs), intent(in) :: tv !< Structure containing pointers to any
1262 !! available thermodynamic fields.
1263 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), &
1264 intent(out) :: U_star !< The surface friction velocity [Z T-1 ~> m s-1]
1265 integer, optional, intent(in) :: halo !< The extra halo size to fill in, 0 by default
1266 logical, optional, intent(in) :: H_T_units !< If present and true, return U_star in units
1267 !! of [H T-1 ~> m s-1 or kg m-2 s-1]
1268
1269 ! Local variables
1270 real :: I_rho ! The inverse of the reference density [R-1 ~> m3 kg-1] or in some semi-Boussinesq cases
1271 ! the rescaled reference density [H2 Z-2 R-1 ~> m3 kg-1 or kg m-3]
1272 logical :: Z_T_units ! If true, U_star is returned in units of [Z T-1 ~> m s-1], otherwise it is
1273 ! returned in [H T-1 ~> m s-1 or kg m-2 s-1]
1274 integer :: i, j, is, ie, js, je, hs
1275
12762160 hs = 0 ; if (present(halo)) hs = max(halo, 0)
12772160 is = G%isc - hs ; ie = G%iec + hs ; js = G%jsc - hs ; je = G%jec + hs
1278
12792160 Z_T_units = .true. ; if (present(H_T_units)) Z_T_units = .not.H_T_units
1280
12812160 if (.not.(associated(forces%ustar) .or. associated(forces%tau_mag))) &
12820 call MOM_error(FATAL, "find_ustar_mech requires that either ustar or tau_mag be associated.")
1283
12842160 if (associated(forces%ustar) .and. (GV%Boussinesq .or. .not.associated(forces%tau_mag))) then
12852160 if (Z_T_units) then
12862160 do concurrent (j=js:je, i=is:ie) !do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
12874274640 U_star(i,j) = forces%ustar(i,j)
1288 enddo
1289 else
12908 do concurrent (j=js:je, i=is:ie) !do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
1291529976 U_star(i,j) = GV%Z_to_H * forces%ustar(i,j)
1292 enddo
1293 endif
12940 elseif (allocated(tv%SpV_avg)) then
12950 if (tv%valid_SpV_halo < 0) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
12960 "find_ustar_mech called in non-Boussinesq mode with invalid values of SpV_avg.")
12970 if (tv%valid_SpV_halo < hs) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
12980 "find_ustar_mech called in non-Boussinesq mode with insufficient valid values of SpV_avg.")
12990 if (Z_T_units) then
13000 do concurrent (j=js:je, i=is:ie)
13010 U_star(i,j) = sqrt(forces%tau_mag(i,j) * tv%SpV_avg(i,j,1))
1302 enddo
1303 else
13040 do concurrent (j=js:je, i=is:ie)
13050 U_star(i,j) = GV%RZ_to_H * sqrt(forces%tau_mag(i,j) / tv%SpV_avg(i,j,1))
1306 enddo
1307 endif
1308 else
13090 I_rho = GV%Z_to_H * GV%RZ_to_H
13100 if (Z_T_units) I_rho = GV%H_to_Z * GV%RZ_to_H ! == 1.0 / GV%Rho0
13110 do concurrent (j=js:je, i=is:ie)
13120 U_star(i,j) = sqrt(forces%tau_mag(i,j) * I_rho)
1313 enddo
1314 endif
1315
13162160end subroutine find_ustar_mech_forcing

1319!> Write out chksums for thermodynamic fluxes.
13200subroutine MOM_forcing_chksum(mesg, fluxes, G, US, haloshift)
1321 character(len=*), intent(in) :: mesg !< message
1322 type(forcing), intent(in) :: fluxes !< A structure containing thermodynamic forcing fields
1323 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< grid type
1324 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
1325 integer, optional, intent(in) :: haloshift !< shift in halo
1326
1327 integer :: hshift
1328
13290 hshift = 1 ; if (present(haloshift)) hshift = haloshift
1330
1331 ! Note that for the chksum calls to be useful for reproducing across PE
1332 ! counts, there must be no redundant points, so all variables use is..ie
1333 ! and js...je as their extent.

13410 call hchksum(fluxes%sw, mesg//" fluxes%sw", G%HI, haloshift=hshift, unscale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)

15400 call locMsg(fluxes%heat_content_cond,'heat_content_massout')
15410 call locMsg(fluxes%heat_content_evap,'heat_content_evap')
15420 call locMsg(fluxes%heat_content_massout,'heat_content_massout')
15430 call locMsg(fluxes%heat_content_massin,'heat_content_massin')
1544
1545 contains
1546 !> Format and write a message depending on associated state of array
15470 subroutine locMsg(array,aname)
1548 real, dimension(:,:), pointer :: array !< Array to write element from
1549 character(len=*) :: aname !< Name of array
1550
15510 if (associated(array)) then
15520 write(0,'(3a,es15.3)') 'MOM_forcing_type, forcing_SinglePointPrint: ',trim(aname),' = ',array(i,j)
1553 else
15540 write(0,'(4a)') 'MOM_forcing_type, forcing_SinglePointPrint: ',trim(aname),' is not associated.'
1555 endif
15560 end subroutine locMsg
1557
1558end subroutine forcing_SinglePointPrint

1561!> Register members of the forcing type for diagnostics
15621subroutine register_forcing_type_diags(Time, diag, US, use_temperature, &
1563 handles, use_berg_fluxes, use_waves, use_cfcs, use_MARBL_tracers, &
1564 use_glc_runoff, use_carbon_runoff)
1565 type(time_type), intent(in) :: Time !< time type
1566 type(diag_ctrl), intent(inout) :: diag !< diagnostic control type
1567 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
1568 logical, intent(in) :: use_temperature !< True if T/S are in use
1569 type(forcing_diags), intent(inout) :: handles !< handles for diagnostics
1570 logical, optional, intent(in) :: use_berg_fluxes !< If true, allow iceberg flux diagnostics
1571 logical, optional, intent(in) :: use_waves !< If true, allow wave forcing diagnostics
1572 logical, optional, intent(in) :: use_cfcs !< If true, allow cfc related diagnostics
1573 logical, optional, intent(in) :: use_MARBL_tracers !< If true, allow MARBL related diagnostics
1574 logical, optional, intent(in) :: use_glc_runoff !< If true, allow separate glacial runoff diagnostics
1575 logical, optional, intent(in) :: use_carbon_runoff !< If true, allow separate carbon runoff diagnostics
1576
