2359
2360!> Accumulate the forcing over time steps, taking input from a mechanical forcing type
2361!! and a temporary forcing-flux type.
23620subroutine forcing_accumulate(flux_tmp, forces, fluxes, G, wt2)
2363 type(forcing), intent(in) :: flux_tmp !< A temporary structure with current
2364 !!thermodynamic forcing fields
2365 type(mech_forcing), intent(in) :: forces !< A structure with the driving mechanical forces
2366 type(forcing), intent(inout) :: fluxes !< A structure containing time-averaged
2367 !! thermodynamic forcing fields
2368 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(inout) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure
2369 real, intent(out) :: wt2 !< The relative weight of the new fluxes [nondim]
2370
2371 ! This subroutine copies mechancal forcing from flux_tmp to fluxes and
2372 ! stores the time-weighted averages of the various buoyancy fluxes in fluxes,
2373 ! and increments the amount of time over which the buoyancy forcing should be
2374 ! applied, all via a call to fluxes accumulate.
2375
23760 call fluxes_accumulate(flux_tmp, fluxes, G, wt2, forces)
2377
23780end subroutine forcing_accumulate
2379
2380!> Accumulate the thermodynamic fluxes over time steps
23810subroutine fluxes_accumulate(flux_tmp, fluxes, G, wt2, forces)
2382 type(forcing), intent(in) :: flux_tmp !< A temporary structure with current
2383 !! thermodynamic forcing fields
2384 type(forcing), intent(inout) :: fluxes !< A structure containing time-averaged
2385 !! thermodynamic forcing fields
2386 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(inout) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure
2387 real, intent(out) :: wt2 !< The relative weight of the new fluxes [nondim]
2388 type(mech_forcing), optional, intent(in) :: forces !< A structure with the driving mechanical forces
2389
2390 ! This subroutine copies mechanical forcing from flux_tmp to fluxes and
2391 ! stores the time-weighted averages of the various buoyancy fluxes in fluxes,
2392 ! and increments the amount of time over which the buoyancy forcing in fluxes should be
2393 ! applied based on the time interval stored in flux_tmp.
2394
2395 real :: wt1 ! The relative weight of the previous fluxes [nondim]
2396 integer :: i, j, is, ie, js, je, Isq, Ieq, Jsq, Jeq, n
2397 integer :: isd, ied, jsd, jed, IsdB, IedB, JsdB, JedB
23980 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec
23990 Isq = G%IscB ; Ieq = G%IecB ; Jsq = G%JscB ; Jeq = G%JecB
24000 isd = G%isd ; ied = G%ied ; jsd = G%jsd ; jed = G%jed
24010 IsdB = G%IsdB ; IedB = G%IedB ; JsdB = G%JsdB ; JedB = G%JedB
2402
2403
24040 if (fluxes%dt_buoy_accum < 0) call MOM_error(FATAL, "fluxes_accumulate: "//&
24050 "fluxes must be initialzed before it can be augmented.")
2406
2407 ! wt1 is the relative weight of the previous fluxes.
24080 wt1 = fluxes%dt_buoy_accum / (fluxes%dt_buoy_accum + flux_tmp%dt_buoy_accum)
24090 wt2 = 1.0 - wt1 ! = flux_tmp%dt_buoy_accum / (fluxes%dt_buoy_accum + flux_tmp%dt_buoy_accum)
24100 fluxes%dt_buoy_accum = fluxes%dt_buoy_accum + flux_tmp%dt_buoy_accum
2411
2412 ! Copy over the pressure fields and accumulate averages of ustar or tau_mag, either from the forcing
2413 ! type or from the temporary fluxes type.
24140 if (present(forces)) then
24150 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24160 fluxes%p_surf(i,j) = forces%p_surf(i,j)
24170 fluxes%p_surf_full(i,j) = forces%p_surf_full(i,j)
2418 enddo ; enddo
2419
24200 if (associated(fluxes%ustar)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24210 fluxes%ustar(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%ustar(i,j) + wt2*forces%ustar(i,j)
2422 enddo ; enddo ; endif
24230 if (associated(fluxes%tau_mag)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24240 fluxes%tau_mag(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%tau_mag(i,j) + wt2*forces%tau_mag(i,j)
2425 enddo ; enddo ; endif
2426 else
24270 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24280 fluxes%p_surf(i,j) = flux_tmp%p_surf(i,j)
24290 fluxes%p_surf_full(i,j) = flux_tmp%p_surf_full(i,j)
2430 enddo ; enddo
2431
24320 if (associated(fluxes%ustar)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24330 fluxes%ustar(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%ustar(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%ustar(i,j)
2434 enddo ; enddo ; endif
24350 if (associated(fluxes%tau_mag)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24360 fluxes%tau_mag(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%tau_mag(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%tau_mag(i,j)
2437 enddo ; enddo ; endif
2438 endif
2439
2440 ! Average ustar_gustless.
24410 if (associated(fluxes%ustar_gustless)) then
24420 if (fluxes%gustless_accum_bug) then
24430 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24440 fluxes%ustar_gustless(i,j) = flux_tmp%ustar_gustless(i,j)
2445 enddo ; enddo
2446 else
24470 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24480 fluxes%ustar_gustless(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%ustar_gustless(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%ustar_gustless(i,j)
2449 enddo ; enddo
2450 endif
2451 endif
2452
24530 if (associated(fluxes%tau_mag_gustless)) then
24540 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24550 fluxes%tau_mag_gustless(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%tau_mag_gustless(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%tau_mag_gustless(i,j)
2456 enddo ; enddo
2457 endif
2458
2459 ! Average the water, heat, and salt fluxes.
24600 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24610 fluxes%evap(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%evap(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%evap(i,j)
24620 fluxes%lprec(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%lprec(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%lprec(i,j)
24630 fluxes%fprec(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%fprec(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%fprec(i,j)
24640 fluxes%vprec(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%vprec(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%vprec(i,j)
24650 fluxes%lrunoff(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%lrunoff(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%lrunoff(i,j)
24660 fluxes%frunoff(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%frunoff(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%frunoff(i,j)
24670 fluxes%lrunoff_glc(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%lrunoff_glc(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%lrunoff_glc(i,j)
24680 fluxes%frunoff_glc(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%frunoff_glc(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%frunoff_glc(i,j)
24690 fluxes%seaice_melt(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%seaice_melt(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%seaice_melt(i,j)
24700 fluxes%sw(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%sw(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%sw(i,j)
24710 fluxes%sw_vis_dir(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%sw_vis_dir(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%sw_vis_dir(i,j)
24720 fluxes%sw_vis_dif(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%sw_vis_dif(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%sw_vis_dif(i,j)
24730 fluxes%sw_nir_dir(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%sw_nir_dir(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%sw_nir_dir(i,j)
24740 fluxes%sw_nir_dif(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%sw_nir_dif(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%sw_nir_dif(i,j)
24750 fluxes%lw(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%lw(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%lw(i,j)
24760 fluxes%latent(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%latent(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%latent(i,j)
24770 fluxes%sens(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%sens(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%sens(i,j)
2478
24790 fluxes%salt_flux(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%salt_flux(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%salt_flux(i,j)
2480 enddo ; enddo
24810 if (associated(fluxes%heat_added) .and. associated(flux_tmp%heat_added)) then
24820 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24830 fluxes%heat_added(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%heat_added(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%heat_added(i,j)
2484 enddo ; enddo
2485 endif
2486 ! These might always be associated, in which case they can be combined?
24870 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_cond) .and. associated(flux_tmp%heat_content_cond)) then
24880 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24890 fluxes%heat_content_cond(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%heat_content_cond(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%heat_content_cond(i,j)
2490 enddo ; enddo
2491 endif
24920 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_evap) .and. associated(flux_tmp%heat_content_evap)) then
24930 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24940 fluxes%heat_content_evap(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%heat_content_evap(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%heat_content_evap(i,j)
2495 enddo ; enddo
2496 endif
24970 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_lprec) .and. associated(flux_tmp%heat_content_lprec)) then
24980 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
24990 fluxes%heat_content_lprec(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%heat_content_lprec(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%heat_content_lprec(i,j)
2500 enddo ; enddo
2501 endif
25020 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_fprec) .and. associated(flux_tmp%heat_content_fprec)) then
25030 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
25040 fluxes%heat_content_fprec(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%heat_content_fprec(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%heat_content_fprec(i,j)
2505 enddo ; enddo
2506 endif
25070 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_vprec) .and. associated(flux_tmp%heat_content_vprec)) then
25080 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
25090 fluxes%heat_content_vprec(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%heat_content_vprec(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%heat_content_vprec(i,j)
2510 enddo ; enddo
2511 endif
25120 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff) .and. associated(flux_tmp%heat_content_lrunoff)) then
25130 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
25140 fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%heat_content_lrunoff(i,j)
2515 enddo ; enddo
2516 endif
25170 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_frunoff) .and. associated(flux_tmp%heat_content_frunoff)) then
25180 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
25190 fluxes%heat_content_frunoff(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%heat_content_frunoff(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%heat_content_frunoff(i,j)
2520 enddo ; enddo
2521 endif
25220 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc) .and. associated(flux_tmp%heat_content_lrunoff_glc)) then
25230 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
2524 fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc(i,j) + &
25250 wt2*flux_tmp%heat_content_lrunoff_glc(i,j)
2526 enddo ; enddo
2527 endif
25280 if (associated(fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc) .and. associated(flux_tmp%heat_content_frunoff_glc)) then
25290 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
2530 fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc(i,j) + &
25310 wt2*flux_tmp%heat_content_frunoff_glc(i,j)
2532 enddo ; enddo
2533 endif
25340 if (associated(fluxes%carbon_content_lrunoff) .and. associated(flux_tmp%carbon_content_lrunoff)) then
25350 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
2536 fluxes%carbon_content_lrunoff(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%carbon_content_lrunoff(i,j) + &
25370 wt2*flux_tmp%carbon_content_lrunoff(i,j)
2538 enddo ; enddo
2539 endif
2540
25410 if (associated(fluxes%ustar_shelf) .and. associated(flux_tmp%ustar_shelf)) then
25420 do i=isd,ied ; do j=jsd,jed
25430 fluxes%ustar_shelf(i,j) = flux_tmp%ustar_shelf(i,j)
2544 enddo ; enddo
2545 endif
25460 if (associated(fluxes%iceshelf_melt) .and. associated(flux_tmp%iceshelf_melt)) then
25470 do i=isd,ied ; do j=jsd,jed
25480 fluxes%iceshelf_melt(i,j) = flux_tmp%iceshelf_melt(i,j)
2549 enddo ; enddo
2550 endif
2551 if (associated(fluxes%shelf_sfc_mass_flux) &
25520 .and. associated(flux_tmp%shelf_sfc_mass_flux)) then
25530 do i=isd,ied ; do j=jsd,jed
25540 fluxes%shelf_sfc_mass_flux(i,j) = flux_tmp%shelf_sfc_mass_flux(i,j)
2555 enddo ; enddo
2556 endif
25570 if (associated(fluxes%frac_shelf_h) .and. associated(flux_tmp%frac_shelf_h)) then
25580 do i=isd,ied ; do j=jsd,jed
25590 fluxes%frac_shelf_h(i,j) = flux_tmp%frac_shelf_h(i,j)
2560 enddo ; enddo
2561 endif
2562
2563 ! Forcings introduced for MARBL
2564 ! NOTE: fluxes%salt_flux, %sw, and %p_surf_full are handled above
25650 if (associated(fluxes%nhx_dep) .and. associated(flux_tmp%nhx_dep)) then
25660 do j=jsd,jed ; do i=isd,ied
25670 fluxes%nhx_dep(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%nhx_dep(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%nhx_dep(i,j)
2568 enddo ; enddo
2569 endif
25700 if (associated(fluxes%noy_dep) .and. associated(flux_tmp%noy_dep)) then
25710 do j=jsd,jed ; do i=isd,ied
25720 fluxes%noy_dep(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%noy_dep(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%noy_dep(i,j)
2573 enddo ; enddo
2574 endif
25750 if (associated(fluxes%atm_co2) .and. associated(flux_tmp%atm_co2)) then
25760 do j=jsd,jed ; do i=isd,ied
25770 fluxes%atm_co2(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%atm_co2(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%atm_co2(i,j)
2578 enddo ; enddo
2579 endif
25800 if (associated(fluxes%atm_alt_co2) .and. associated(flux_tmp%atm_alt_co2)) then
25810 do j=jsd,jed ; do i=isd,ied
25820 fluxes%atm_alt_co2(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%atm_alt_co2(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%atm_alt_co2(i,j)
2583 enddo ; enddo
2584 endif
25850 if (associated(fluxes%dust_flux) .and. associated(flux_tmp%dust_flux)) then
25860 do j=jsd,jed ; do i=isd,ied
25870 fluxes%dust_flux(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%dust_flux(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%dust_flux(i,j)
2588 enddo ; enddo
2589 endif
25900 if (associated(fluxes%iron_flux) .and. associated(flux_tmp%iron_flux)) then
25910 do j=jsd,jed ; do i=isd,ied
25920 fluxes%iron_flux(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%iron_flux(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%iron_flux(i,j)
2593 enddo ; enddo
2594 endif
25950 if (associated(fluxes%atm_fine_dust_flux) .and. associated(flux_tmp%atm_fine_dust_flux)) then

2598 enddo ; enddo
2599 endif
26000 if (associated(fluxes%atm_coarse_dust_flux) .and. associated(flux_tmp%atm_coarse_dust_flux)) then

26070 fluxes%atm_bc_flux(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%atm_bc_flux(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%atm_bc_flux(i,j)
2608 enddo ; enddo
2609 endif
26100 if (associated(fluxes%seaice_dust_flux) .and. associated(flux_tmp%seaice_dust_flux)) then

2613 enddo ; enddo

2618 enddo ; enddo
2619 endif
26200 if (associated(fluxes%fracr_cat) .and. associated(flux_tmp%fracr_cat)) then
26210 do n=1,size(fluxes%fracr_cat,dim=3) ; do j=jsd,jed ; do i=isd,ied

2624 endif

2628 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
2629 endif
26300 if (associated(fluxes%ice_fraction) .and. associated(flux_tmp%ice_fraction)) then
26310 do j=jsd,jed ; do i=isd,ied
26320 fluxes%ice_fraction(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%ice_fraction(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%ice_fraction(i,j)
2633 enddo ; enddo
2634 endif

26360 do j=jsd,jed ; do i=isd,ied
26370 fluxes%u10_sqr(i,j) = wt1*fluxes%u10_sqr(i,j) + wt2*flux_tmp%u10_sqr(i,j)
2638 enddo ; enddo
2639 endif
2640
26410 if (coupler_type_initialized(fluxes%tr_fluxes) .and. &
2642 coupler_type_initialized(flux_tmp%tr_fluxes)) &
2643 call coupler_type_increment_data(flux_tmp%tr_fluxes, fluxes%tr_fluxes, &
26440 scale_factor=wt2, scale_prev=wt1)
2645
26460end subroutine fluxes_accumulate

2648!> This subroutine copies the computational domains of common forcing fields

2700!> This subroutine calculates certain derived forcing fields based on information
2701!! from a mech_forcing type and stores them in a (thermodynamic) forcing type.
270212subroutine set_derived_forcing_fields(forces, fluxes, G, US, Rho0)
2703 type(mech_forcing), intent(in) :: forces !< A structure with the driving mechanical forces
2704 type(forcing), intent(inout) :: fluxes !< A structure containing thermodynamic forcing fields
2705 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< grid type
2706 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
2707 real, intent(in) :: Rho0 !< A reference density of seawater [R ~> kg m-3],
2708 !! as used to calculate ustar.
2709
2710 real :: taux2, tauy2 ! Squared wind stress components [R2 L2 Z2 T-4 ~> Pa2].
2711 real :: Irho0 ! Inverse of the mean density rescaled to [Z L-1 R-1 ~> m3 kg-1]
2712 integer :: i, j, is, ie, js, je
271312 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec
2714
271512 Irho0 = US%L_to_Z / Rho0
2716
271712 if ( associated(forces%taux) .and. associated(forces%tauy) .and. &
2718 (associated(fluxes%ustar_gustless) .or. associated(fluxes%tau_mag_gustless)) ) then
271987132 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
272086400 taux2 = 0.0
272186400 if ((G%mask2dCu(I-1,j) + G%mask2dCu(I,j)) > 0.0) &
2722 taux2 = (G%mask2dCu(I-1,j) * (forces%taux(I-1,j)**2) + &
2723 G%mask2dCu(I,j) * (forces%taux(I,j)**2)) / &
272460168 (G%mask2dCu(I-1,j) + G%mask2dCu(I,j))
272586400 tauy2 = 0.0
272686400 if ((G%mask2dCv(i,J-1) + G%mask2dCv(i,J)) > 0.0) &
2727 tauy2 = (G%mask2dCv(i,J-1) * (forces%tauy(i,J-1)**2) + &
2728 G%mask2dCv(i,J) * (forces%tauy(i,J)**2)) / &
272960168 (G%mask2dCv(i,J-1) + G%mask2dCv(i,J))
2730
273186400 if (associated(fluxes%ustar_gustless)) then
273286400 if (fluxes%gustless_accum_bug) then
2733 ! This change is just for computational efficiency, but it is wrapped with another change.
27340 fluxes%ustar_gustless(i,j) = sqrt(US%L_to_Z * sqrt(taux2 + tauy2) / Rho0)
2735 else
273686400 fluxes%ustar_gustless(i,j) = sqrt(sqrt(taux2 + tauy2) * Irho0)
2737 endif
2738 endif
273987120 if (associated(fluxes%tau_mag_gustless)) then
27400 fluxes%tau_mag_gustless(i,j) = US%L_to_Z*sqrt(taux2 + tauy2)
2741 endif
2742 enddo ; enddo
2743 endif
2744
274512end subroutine set_derived_forcing_fields

2761!> This subroutine calculates determines the net mass source to the ocean from
2762!! a (thermodynamic) forcing type and stores it in a provided array.
276312subroutine get_net_mass_forcing(fluxes, G, US, net_mass_src)
2764 type(forcing), intent(in) :: fluxes !< A structure containing thermodynamic forcing fields
2765 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean grid type
2766 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
2767 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(out) :: net_mass_src !< The net mass flux of water into the ocean
2768 !! [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1].
2769
2770 integer :: i, j, is, ie, js, je
277112 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec
2772
2773105276 net_mass_src(:,:) = 0.0
277487132 if (associated(fluxes%lprec)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
277587120 net_mass_src(i,j) = net_mass_src(i,j) + fluxes%lprec(i,j)
2776 enddo ; enddo ; endif
277787132 if (associated(fluxes%fprec)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
277887120 net_mass_src(i,j) = net_mass_src(i,j) + fluxes%fprec(i,j)
2779 enddo ; enddo ; endif
278087132 if (associated(fluxes%vprec)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
278187120 net_mass_src(i,j) = net_mass_src(i,j) + fluxes%vprec(i,j)
2782 enddo ; enddo ; endif
278387132 if (associated(fluxes%lrunoff)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
278487120 net_mass_src(i,j) = net_mass_src(i,j) + fluxes%lrunoff(i,j)
2785 enddo ; enddo ; endif
278687132 if (associated(fluxes%frunoff)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
278787120 net_mass_src(i,j) = net_mass_src(i,j) + fluxes%frunoff(i,j)
2788 enddo ; enddo ; endif
278987132 if (associated(fluxes%lrunoff_glc)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
279087120 net_mass_src(i,j) = net_mass_src(i,j) + fluxes%lrunoff_glc(i,j)
2791 enddo ; enddo ; endif

279587132 if (associated(fluxes%evap)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
279687120 net_mass_src(i,j) = net_mass_src(i,j) + fluxes%evap(i,j)
2797 enddo ; enddo ; endif
279887132 if (associated(fluxes%seaice_melt)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
279987120 net_mass_src(i,j) = net_mass_src(i,j) + fluxes%seaice_melt(i,j)
2800 enddo ; enddo ; endif

2889!> Offer buoyancy forcing fields for diagnostics for those
2890!! fields registered as part of register_forcing_type_diags.
2891360subroutine forcing_diagnostics(fluxes_in, sfc_state, G_in, US, time_end, diag, handles, enthalpy)
2892 type(forcing), target, intent(in) :: fluxes_in !< A structure containing thermodynamic forcing fields
2893 type(surface), intent(in) :: sfc_state !< A structure containing fields that
2894 !! describe the surface state of the ocean.
2895 type(ocean_grid_type), target, intent(in) :: G_in !< Input grid type
2896 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
2897 type(time_type), intent(in) :: time_end !< The end time of the diagnostic interval.
2898 type(diag_ctrl), intent(inout) :: diag !< diagnostic regulator
2899 type(forcing_diags), intent(inout) :: handles !< diagnostic ids
2900 logical, optional, intent(in ) :: enthalpy !< If present and true, the heat content associated
2901 !! with mass entering/leaving the ocean is provided
2902 !! by the coupler. Diagnostics net_heat_surface and
2903 !! heat_content_surfwater are computed using
2904 !! heat_content_evap instead of heat_content_massout.
2905
2906 ! local variables
2907 type(ocean_grid_type), pointer :: G ! Grid metric on model index map
2908 type(forcing), pointer :: fluxes ! Fluxes on the model index map
2909720 real, dimension(SZI_(diag%G),SZJ_(diag%G)) :: res ! A temporary array for combinations
2910 ! of mass fluxes [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1] or heat fluxes [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
2911 real :: total_mass_flux ! Diagnostic of an integrated boundary mass flux in [R Z L2 T-1 ~> kg s-1]
2912 real :: total_heat_flux ! Diagnostic of an integrated boundary heat flux in [Q R Z L2 T-1 ~> W]
2913 real :: total_salt_flux ! Diagnostic of an integrated boundary salt flux in [R Z L2 T-1 ~> kg s-1]
2914 real :: ave_mass_flux ! Diagnostic of the average of a surface mass flux in [R Z T-1 ~> kg m-2 s-1]
2915 real :: ave_heat_flux ! Diagnostic of the average of a surface heat flux in [Q R Z T-1 ~> W m-2]
2916 real :: I_dt ! inverse time step [T-1 ~> s-1]
2917 integer :: turns ! Number of index quarter turns
2918 logical :: mom_enthalpy ! If true (default) enthalpy terms are computed in MOM6
2919 integer :: i, j, is, ie, js, je
2920
2921360 call cpu_clock_begin(handles%id_clock_forcing)
2922
2923360 mom_enthalpy = .true. ; if (present(enthalpy)) mom_enthalpy = .not. enthalpy
2924

2926 ! rotations must be applied before saving the output.
2927360 turns = diag%G%HI%turns
2928360 if (turns /= 0) then
29290 G => diag%G
29300 allocate(fluxes)
29310 call allocate_forcing_type(fluxes_in, G, fluxes, turns=turns)
29320 call rotate_forcing(fluxes_in, fluxes, turns)
2933 else
2934360 G => G_in
2935360 fluxes => fluxes_in
2936 endif
2937
2938360 I_dt = 1.0 / fluxes%dt_buoy_accum
2939360 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec
2940
2941360 call enable_averages(fluxes%dt_buoy_accum, time_end, diag)
2942 ! if (query_averaging_enabled(diag)) then
2943
2944 ! post the diagnostics for surface mass fluxes ==================================
2945
2946360 if (handles%id_prcme > 0 .or. handles%id_total_prcme > 0 .or. handles%id_prcme_ga > 0) then
29470 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
29480 res(i,j) = 0.0
29490 if (associated(fluxes%lprec)) res(i,j) = res(i,j) + fluxes%lprec(i,j)
29500 if (associated(fluxes%fprec)) res(i,j) = res(i,j) + fluxes%fprec(i,j)
2951 ! fluxes%cond is not needed because it is derived from %evap > 0
29520 if (associated(fluxes%evap)) res(i,j) = res(i,j) + fluxes%evap(i,j)

2991 endif
2992 endif

33710 ave_heat_flux = global_area_mean(fluxes%sw, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
33720 call post_data(handles%id_sw_ga, ave_heat_flux, diag)
3373 endif
3374
3375360 if ((handles%id_lw > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%lw)) then
33760 call post_data(handles%id_lw, fluxes%lw, diag)
3377 endif
3378360 if ((handles%id_total_lw > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%lw)) then
33790 total_heat_flux = global_area_integral(fluxes%lw, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
33800 call post_data(handles%id_total_lw, total_heat_flux, diag)
3381 endif
3382360 if ((handles%id_lw_ga > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%lw)) then
33830 ave_heat_flux = global_area_mean(fluxes%lw, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)

3385 endif

34680 total_salt_flux = global_area_integral(fluxes%salt_flux, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)

34730 call post_data(handles%id_saltFluxAdded, fluxes%salt_flux_added, diag)

34750 total_salt_flux = global_area_integral(fluxes%salt_flux_added, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)

3510360 if ((handles%id_atm_fine_dust_flux > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%atm_fine_dust_flux)) &

3512
3513360 if ((handles%id_atm_coarse_dust_flux > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%atm_coarse_dust_flux)) &

3516360 if ((handles%id_atm_bc_flux > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%atm_bc_flux)) &

3518
3519360 if ((handles%id_seaice_dust_flux > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%seaice_dust_flux)) &

3521
3522360 if ((handles%id_seaice_bc_flux > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%seaice_bc_flux)) &
35230 call post_data(handles%id_seaice_bc_flux, fluxes%seaice_bc_flux, diag)

3526
3527360 if ((handles%id_psurf > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%p_surf)) &
35280 call post_data(handles%id_psurf, fluxes%p_surf, diag)

3533360 if ((handles%id_buoy > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%buoy)) &
35340 call post_data(handles%id_buoy, fluxes%buoy, diag)
3535
3536360 if ((handles%id_tau_mag > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%tau_mag)) &
35370 call post_data(handles%id_tau_mag, fluxes%tau_mag, diag)
3538
3539360 if ((handles%id_ustar > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%ustar)) &
35400 call post_data(handles%id_ustar, fluxes%ustar, diag)
3541
3542360 if ((handles%id_omega_w2x > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%omega_w2x)) &
35430 call post_data(handles%id_omega_w2x, fluxes%omega_w2x, diag)
3544
3545360 if ((handles%id_ustar_berg > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%ustar_berg)) &
35460 call post_data(handles%id_ustar_berg, fluxes%ustar_berg, diag)
3547
3548360 if ((handles%id_frac_ice_cover > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%frac_shelf_h)) &

3550
3551360 if ((handles%id_ustar_ice_cover > 0) .and. associated(fluxes%ustar_shelf)) &
35520 call post_data(handles%id_ustar_ice_cover, fluxes%ustar_shelf, diag)
3553
3554 ! wave forcing ===============================================================
3555360 if (handles%id_lamult > 0) &
35560 call post_data(handles%id_lamult, fluxes%lamult, diag)
3557
3558 ! endif ! query_averaging_enabled
3559360 call disable_averaging(diag)
3560
3561360 if (turns /= 0) then
35620 call deallocate_forcing_type(fluxes)
35630 deallocate(fluxes)
3564 endif
3565
3566360 call cpu_clock_end(handles%id_clock_forcing)
3567360end subroutine forcing_diagnostics

3570!> Conditionally allocate fields within the forcing type
35712subroutine allocate_forcing_by_group(G, fluxes, water, heat, ustar, press, &
3572 shelf, iceberg, salt, fix_accum_bug, cfc, marbl, &
3573 waves, shelf_sfc_accumulation, lamult, hevap, &
3574 ice_ncat, tau_mag, carbon)
3575 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid structure
3576 type(forcing), intent(inout) :: fluxes !< A structure containing thermodynamic forcing fields
3577 logical, optional, intent(in) :: water !< If present and true, allocate water fluxes
3578 logical, optional, intent(in) :: heat !< If present and true, allocate heat fluxes
3579 logical, optional, intent(in) :: ustar !< If present and true, allocate ustar and related fields
3580 logical, optional, intent(in) :: press !< If present and true, allocate p_surf and related fields
3581 logical, optional, intent(in) :: shelf !< If present and true, allocate fluxes for ice-shelf
3582 logical, optional, intent(in) :: iceberg !< If present and true, allocate fluxes for icebergs
3583 logical, optional, intent(in) :: salt !< If present and true, allocate salt fluxes
3584 logical, optional, intent(in) :: fix_accum_bug !< If present and true, avoid using a bug in
3585 !! accumulation of ustar_gustless
3586 logical, optional, intent(in) :: cfc !< If present and true, allocate fields needed
3587 !! for cfc surface fluxes
3588 logical, optional, intent(in) :: marbl !< If present and true, allocate fields needed
3589 !! for MARBL surface fluxes
3590 logical, optional, intent(in) :: waves !< If present and true, allocate wave fields
3591 logical, optional, intent(in) :: shelf_sfc_accumulation !< If present and true, and shelf is true,
3592 !! then allocate surface flux deposition from the atmosphere
3593 !! over ice shelves and ice sheets.
3594 logical, optional, intent(in) :: lamult !< If present and true, allocate langmuir enhancement factor
3595 logical, optional, intent(in) :: hevap !< If present and true, allocate heat content evap.
3596 !! This field must be allocated when enthalpy is provided
3597 !! via coupler.
3598 integer, optional, intent(in) :: ice_ncat !< number of ice categories
3599 logical, optional, intent(in) :: tau_mag !< If present and true, allocate tau_mag and related fields
3600 logical, optional, intent(in) :: carbon !< If present and true, allocate carbon fluxes
3601
3602 ! Local variables
3603 integer :: isd, ied, jsd, jed, IsdB, IedB, JsdB, JedB
3604 logical :: shelf_sfc_acc, enthalpy_mom
3605
3606 ! if true, allocate fluxes needed to calculate enthalpy terms in MOM6
36072 enthalpy_mom = .true.
36082 if (present (hevap)) enthalpy_mom = .not. hevap
3609
36102 isd = G%isd ; ied = G%ied ; jsd = G%jsd ; jed = G%jed
36112 IsdB = G%IsdB ; IedB = G%IedB ; JsdB = G%JsdB ; JedB = G%JedB
3612
36132 call myAlloc(fluxes%ustar,isd,ied,jsd,jed, ustar)
36142 call myAlloc(fluxes%ustar_gustless,isd,ied,jsd,jed, ustar)
36152 call myAlloc(fluxes%tau_mag,isd,ied,jsd,jed, ustar)
3616
3617 ! Note that myAlloc can be called safely multiple times for the same pointer.
36182 call myAlloc(fluxes%tau_mag,isd,ied,jsd,jed, tau_mag)
36192 call myAlloc(fluxes%tau_mag_gustless,isd,ied,jsd,jed, tau_mag)
3620
36212 call myAlloc(fluxes%evap,isd,ied,jsd,jed, water)
36222 call myAlloc(fluxes%lprec,isd,ied,jsd,jed, water)
36232 call myAlloc(fluxes%fprec,isd,ied,jsd,jed, water)
36242 call myAlloc(fluxes%vprec,isd,ied,jsd,jed, water)
36252 call myAlloc(fluxes%lrunoff,isd,ied,jsd,jed, water)
36262 call myAlloc(fluxes%frunoff,isd,ied,jsd,jed, water)
36272 call myAlloc(fluxes%lrunoff_glc,isd,ied,jsd,jed, water)
36282 call myAlloc(fluxes%frunoff_glc,isd,ied,jsd,jed, water)
36292 call myAlloc(fluxes%seaice_melt,isd,ied,jsd,jed, water)
36302 call myAlloc(fluxes%netMassOut,isd,ied,jsd,jed, water)
36312 call myAlloc(fluxes%netMassIn,isd,ied,jsd,jed, water)
36322 call myAlloc(fluxes%seaice_melt_heat,isd,ied,jsd,jed, heat)
36332 call myAlloc(fluxes%sw,isd,ied,jsd,jed, heat)
36342 call myAlloc(fluxes%lw,isd,ied,jsd,jed, heat)
36352 call myAlloc(fluxes%latent,isd,ied,jsd,jed, heat)
36362 call myAlloc(fluxes%sens,isd,ied,jsd,jed, heat)
36372 call myAlloc(fluxes%latent_evap_diag,isd,ied,jsd,jed, heat)
36382 call myAlloc(fluxes%latent_fprec_diag,isd,ied,jsd,jed, heat)
36392 call myAlloc(fluxes%latent_frunoff_diag,isd,ied,jsd,jed, heat)
36402 call myAlloc(fluxes%latent_frunoff_glc_diag,isd,ied,jsd,jed, heat)
3641
36422 call myAlloc(fluxes%salt_flux,isd,ied,jsd,jed, salt)
36432 call myAlloc(fluxes%carbon_content_lrunoff,isd,ied,jsd,jed, carbon)
3644
36452 if (present(heat) .and. present(water)) then ; if (heat .and. water) then
36461 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_content_cond,isd,ied,jsd,jed, .true.)
36471 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_content_evap,isd,ied,jsd,jed, .not. enthalpy_mom)
36481 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_content_lprec,isd,ied,jsd,jed, .true.)
36491 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_content_fprec,isd,ied,jsd,jed, .true.)
36501 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_content_vprec,isd,ied,jsd,jed, .true.)
36511 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff,isd,ied,jsd,jed, .true.)
36521 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_content_frunoff,isd,ied,jsd,jed, .true.)
36531 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc,isd,ied,jsd,jed, .true.)
36541 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc,isd,ied,jsd,jed, .true.)
36551 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_content_massout,isd,ied,jsd,jed, enthalpy_mom)
36561 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_content_massin,isd,ied,jsd,jed, enthalpy_mom)
3657 endif ; endif
3658
36592 call myAlloc(fluxes%p_surf,isd,ied,jsd,jed, press)
3660
3661 ! These fields should only be allocated if ice shelf is enabled.
36622 if (present(shelf)) then ; if (shelf) then
36630 call myAlloc(fluxes%frac_shelf_h,isd,ied,jsd,jed, shelf)
36640 call myAlloc(fluxes%ustar_shelf,isd,ied,jsd,jed, shelf)
36650 call myAlloc(fluxes%iceshelf_melt,isd,ied,jsd,jed, shelf)
36660 shelf_sfc_acc = .false. ; if (present(shelf_sfc_accumulation)) shelf_sfc_acc = shelf_sfc_accumulation
36670 if (shelf_sfc_acc) call myAlloc(fluxes%shelf_sfc_mass_flux,isd,ied,jsd,jed, shelf_sfc_acc)
3668 endif ; endif
3669
3670 !These fields should only be allocated when iceberg area is being passed through the coupler.
36712 call myAlloc(fluxes%ustar_berg,isd,ied,jsd,jed, iceberg)
36722 call myAlloc(fluxes%area_berg,isd,ied,jsd,jed, iceberg)
36732 call myAlloc(fluxes%mass_berg,isd,ied,jsd,jed, iceberg)
3674
3675 !These fields should only be allocated when USE_CFC_CAP is activated.
36762 call myAlloc(fluxes%ice_fraction,isd,ied,jsd,jed, cfc)
36772 call myAlloc(fluxes%u10_sqr,isd,ied,jsd,jed, cfc)
3678
3679 !These fields should only be allocated when wave coupling is activated.
36802 call myAlloc(fluxes%ice_fraction,isd,ied,jsd,jed, waves)
36812 call myAlloc(fluxes%lamult,isd,ied,jsd,jed, lamult)
3682
36832 if (present(fix_accum_bug)) fluxes%gustless_accum_bug = .not.fix_accum_bug
3684
3685 !These fields should only be allocated when USE_MARBL_TRACERS is activated.
36862 call myAlloc(fluxes%ice_fraction,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36872 call myAlloc(fluxes%u10_sqr,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36882 call myAlloc(fluxes%noy_dep,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36892 call myAlloc(fluxes%nhx_dep,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36902 call myAlloc(fluxes%atm_co2,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36912 call myAlloc(fluxes%atm_alt_co2,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36922 call myAlloc(fluxes%dust_flux,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36932 call myAlloc(fluxes%iron_flux,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36942 call myAlloc(fluxes%atm_fine_dust_flux,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36952 call myAlloc(fluxes%atm_coarse_dust_flux,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36962 call myAlloc(fluxes%atm_bc_flux,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36972 call myAlloc(fluxes%seaice_dust_flux,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
36982 call myAlloc(fluxes%seaice_bc_flux,isd,ied,jsd,jed, marbl)
3699
3700 ! These fields should only be allocated when receiving multiple ice categories
37012 if (present(ice_ncat)) then
37020 call myAlloc(fluxes%fracr_cat,isd,ied,jsd,jed,1,ice_ncat+1, ice_ncat > 0)
37030 call myAlloc(fluxes%qsw_cat,isd,ied,jsd,jed,1,ice_ncat+1, ice_ncat > 0)
3704 endif
3705
37062end subroutine allocate_forcing_by_group
3707
3708!> Allocate elements of a new forcing type based on their status in an existing type.
37090subroutine allocate_forcing_by_ref(fluxes_ref, G, fluxes, turns)
3710 type(forcing), intent(in) :: fluxes_ref !< Reference fluxes
3711 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Grid metric of target fluxes
3712 type(forcing), intent(out) :: fluxes !< Target fluxes
3713 integer, optional, intent(in) :: turns !< If present, the number of counterclockwise
3714 !! quarter turns to use on the new grid.
3715
3716 logical :: do_ustar, do_taumag, do_water, do_heat, do_salt, do_press, do_shelf
3717 logical :: do_iceberg, do_heat_added, do_buoy, do_carbon
3718 logical :: even_turns ! True if turns is absent or even
3719
3720 call get_forcing_groups(fluxes_ref, do_water, do_heat, do_ustar, do_taumag, do_press, &
37210 do_shelf, do_iceberg, do_salt, do_heat_added, do_buoy, do_carbon)
3722
3723 call allocate_forcing_type(G, fluxes, do_water, do_heat, do_ustar, &
37240 do_press, do_shelf, do_iceberg, do_salt, tau_mag=do_taumag, carbon=do_carbon)
3725
3726 ! The following fluxes would typically be allocated by the driver
3727 call myAlloc(fluxes%sw_vis_dir, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
37280 associated(fluxes_ref%sw_vis_dir))
3729 call myAlloc(fluxes%sw_vis_dif, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
37300 associated(fluxes_ref%sw_vis_dif))
3731 call myAlloc(fluxes%sw_nir_dir, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
37320 associated(fluxes_ref%sw_nir_dir))
3733 call myAlloc(fluxes%sw_nir_dif, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
37340 associated(fluxes_ref%sw_nir_dif))
3735
3736 call myAlloc(fluxes%salt_flux_in, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
37370 associated(fluxes_ref%salt_flux_in))
3738 call myAlloc(fluxes%salt_flux_added, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
37390 associated(fluxes_ref%salt_flux_added))
3740
3741 call myAlloc(fluxes%p_surf_full, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
37420 associated(fluxes_ref%p_surf_full))
3743
3744 call myAlloc(fluxes%heat_added, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
37450 associated(fluxes_ref%heat_added))
3746 call myAlloc(fluxes%buoy, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
37470 associated(fluxes_ref%buoy))
3748
3749 call myAlloc(fluxes%BBL_tidal_dis, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
37500 associated(fluxes_ref%BBL_tidal_dis))
3751 call myAlloc(fluxes%ustar_tidal, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
37520 associated(fluxes_ref%ustar_tidal))
3753
3754 ! This flag would normally be set by a control flag in allocate_forcing_type.
3755 ! Here we copy the flag from the reference forcing.
37560 fluxes%gustless_accum_bug = fluxes_ref%gustless_accum_bug
3757
37580 if (coupler_type_initialized(fluxes_ref%tr_fluxes)) then
3759 ! The data fields in the coupler_2d_bc_type are never rotated.
37600 even_turns = .true. ; if (present(turns)) even_turns = (modulo(turns, 2) == 0)
37610 if (even_turns) then
3762 call coupler_type_spawn(fluxes_ref%tr_fluxes, fluxes%tr_fluxes, &
37630 (/G%isc,G%isc,G%iec,G%iec/), (/G%jsc,G%jsc,G%jec,G%jec/))
3764 else
3765 call coupler_type_spawn(fluxes_ref%tr_fluxes, fluxes%tr_fluxes, &
37660 (/G%jsc,G%jsc,G%jec,G%jec/), (/G%isc,G%isc,G%iec,G%iec/))
3767 endif
3768 endif
3769
37700end subroutine allocate_forcing_by_ref
3771
3772
3773!> Conditionally allocate fields within the mechanical forcing type using
3774!! control flags.
37751subroutine allocate_mech_forcing_by_group(G, forces, stress, ustar, shelf, &
3776 press, iceberg, waves, num_stk_bands, tau_mag)
3777 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid structure
3778 type(mech_forcing), intent(inout) :: forces !< Forcing fields structure
3779
3780 logical, optional, intent(in) :: stress !< If present and true, allocate taux, tauy
3781 logical, optional, intent(in) :: ustar !< If present and true, allocate ustar and related fields
3782 logical, optional, intent(in) :: shelf !< If present and true, allocate forces for ice-shelf
3783 logical, optional, intent(in) :: press !< If present and true, allocate p_surf and related fields
3784 logical, optional, intent(in) :: iceberg !< If present and true, allocate forces for icebergs
3785 logical, optional, intent(in) :: waves !< If present and true, allocate wave fields
3786 integer, optional, intent(in) :: num_stk_bands !< Number of Stokes bands to allocate
3787 logical, optional, intent(in) :: tau_mag !< If present and true, allocate tau_mag
3788
3789 ! Local variables
3790 integer :: isd, ied, jsd, jed, IsdB, IedB, JsdB, JedB
3791
37921 isd = G%isd ; ied = G%ied ; jsd = G%jsd ; jed = G%jed
37931 IsdB = G%IsdB ; IedB = G%IedB ; JsdB = G%JsdB ; JedB = G%JedB
3794
37951 call myAlloc(forces%taux,IsdB,IedB,jsd,jed, stress)
37961 call myAlloc(forces%tauy,isd,ied,JsdB,JedB, stress)
3797
37981 call myAlloc(forces%ustar,isd,ied,jsd,jed, ustar)
37991 call myAlloc(forces%tau_mag,isd,ied,jsd,jed, ustar)
3800 ! Note that myAlloc can be called safely multiple times for the same pointer.
38011 call myAlloc(forces%tau_mag,isd,ied,jsd,jed, tau_mag)
3802
38031 call myAlloc(forces%p_surf,isd,ied,jsd,jed, press)
38041 call myAlloc(forces%p_surf_full,isd,ied,jsd,jed, press)
38051 call myAlloc(forces%net_mass_src,isd,ied,jsd,jed, press)
3806
38071 call myAlloc(forces%rigidity_ice_u,IsdB,IedB,jsd,jed, shelf)
38081 call myAlloc(forces%rigidity_ice_v,isd,ied,JsdB,JedB, shelf)
38091 call myAlloc(forces%frac_shelf_u,IsdB,IedB,jsd,jed, shelf)
38101 call myAlloc(forces%frac_shelf_v,isd,ied,JsdB,JedB, shelf)
3811
3812 !These fields should only on allocated when iceberg area is being passed through the coupler.
38131 call myAlloc(forces%area_berg,isd,ied,jsd,jed, iceberg)
38141 call myAlloc(forces%mass_berg,isd,ied,jsd,jed, iceberg)
3815
3816 !These fields should only be allocated when waves
38171 if (present(waves)) then ; if (waves) then
38180 if (.not. present(num_stk_bands)) then
3819 call MOM_error(FATAL,"Requested to &
38200 &initialize with waves, but no waves are present.")
3821 endif
38220 if (num_stk_bands > 0) then
38230 if (.not.associated(forces%ustkb)) then
38240 allocate(forces%stk_wavenumbers(num_stk_bands), source=0.0)
38250 allocate(forces%ustkb(isd:ied,jsd:jed,num_stk_bands), source=0.0)
38260 allocate(forces%vstkb(isd:ied,jsd:jed,num_stk_bands), source=0.0)
3827 endif
3828 endif
3829 endif ; endif
3830
38311end subroutine allocate_mech_forcing_by_group
3832

3834!> Conditionally allocate fields within the mechanical forcing type based on a
3835!! reference forcing.
38360subroutine allocate_mech_forcing_from_ref(forces_ref, G, forces)
3837 type(mech_forcing), intent(in) :: forces_ref !< Reference forcing fields
3838 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Grid metric of target forcing
3839 type(mech_forcing), intent(out) :: forces !< Mechanical forcing fields
3840
3841 logical :: do_stress, do_ustar, do_tau_mag, do_shelf, do_press, do_iceberg
3842
3843 ! Identify the active fields in the reference forcing
3844 call get_mech_forcing_groups(forces_ref, do_stress, do_ustar, do_tau_mag, do_shelf, &
38450 do_press, do_iceberg)
3846
3847 call allocate_mech_forcing(G, forces, do_stress, do_ustar, do_shelf, &
38480 do_press, do_iceberg, tau_mag=do_tau_mag)
38490end subroutine allocate_mech_forcing_from_ref

3852!> Return flags indicating which groups of forcings are allocated
38530subroutine get_forcing_groups(fluxes, water, heat, ustar, tau_mag, press, shelf, &
3854 iceberg, salt, heat_added, buoy, carbon)
3855 type(forcing), intent(in) :: fluxes !< Reference flux fields
3856 logical, intent(out) :: water !< True if fluxes contains water-based fluxes
3857 logical, intent(out) :: heat !< True if fluxes contains heat-based fluxes
3858 logical, intent(out) :: ustar !< True if fluxes contains ustar
3859 logical, intent(out) :: tau_mag !< True if fluxes contains tau_mag
3860 logical, intent(out) :: press !< True if fluxes contains surface pressure
3861 logical, intent(out) :: shelf !< True if fluxes contains ice shelf fields
3862 logical, intent(out) :: iceberg !< True if fluxes contains iceberg fluxes
3863 logical, intent(out) :: salt !< True if fluxes contains salt flux
3864 logical, intent(out) :: heat_added !< True if fluxes contains explicit heat
3865 logical, intent(out) :: buoy !< True if fluxes contains buoyancy fluxes
3866 logical, optional, intent(out) :: carbon !< True if fluxes contains carbon fluxes
3867
3868 ! NOTE: heat, salt, heat_added, and buoy would typically depend on each other
3869 ! to some degree. But since this would be enforced at the driver level,
3870 ! we handle them here as independent flags.
3871
38720 ustar = associated(fluxes%ustar) .and. associated(fluxes%ustar_gustless)
38730 tau_mag = associated(fluxes%tau_mag) .and. associated(fluxes%tau_mag_gustless)
3874 ! TODO: Check for all associated fields, but for now just check one as a marker
38750 water = associated(fluxes%evap)
38760 heat = associated(fluxes%seaice_melt_heat)
38770 salt = associated(fluxes%salt_flux)
38780 press = associated(fluxes%p_surf)
38790 shelf = associated(fluxes%frac_shelf_h)
38800 iceberg = associated(fluxes%ustar_berg)
38810 heat_added = associated(fluxes%heat_added)
38820 buoy = associated(fluxes%buoy)
38830 if (present(carbon)) carbon = associated(fluxes%carbon_content_lrunoff)
38840end subroutine get_forcing_groups
3885
3886
3887!> Return flags indicating which groups of mechanical forcings are allocated
38880subroutine get_mech_forcing_groups(forces, stress, ustar, tau_mag, shelf, press, iceberg)
3889 type(mech_forcing), intent(in) :: forces !< Reference forcing fields
3890 logical, intent(out) :: stress !< True if forces contains wind stress fields
3891 logical, intent(out) :: ustar !< True if forces contains ustar field
3892 logical, intent(out) :: tau_mag !< True if forces contains tau_mag field
3893 logical, intent(out) :: shelf !< True if forces contains ice shelf fields
3894 logical, intent(out) :: press !< True if forces contains pressure fields
3895 logical, intent(out) :: iceberg !< True if forces contains iceberg fields
3896
3897 stress = associated(forces%taux) &
38980 .and. associated(forces%tauy)
38990 ustar = associated(forces%ustar)
39000 tau_mag = associated(forces%tau_mag)
3901 shelf = associated(forces%rigidity_ice_u) &
3902 .and. associated(forces%rigidity_ice_v) &
3903 .and. associated(forces%frac_shelf_u) &
39040 .and. associated(forces%frac_shelf_v)
3905 press = associated(forces%p_surf) &
3906 .and. associated(forces%p_surf_full) &
39070 .and. associated(forces%net_mass_src)
3908 iceberg = associated(forces%area_berg) &
39090 .and. associated(forces%mass_berg)
39100end subroutine get_mech_forcing_groups

3914121subroutine myAlloc_2d(array, is, ie, js, je, flag)
3915 real, dimension(:,:), pointer :: array !< Array to be allocated
3916 integer, intent(in) :: is !< Start i-index
3917 integer, intent(in) :: ie !< End i-index
3918 integer, intent(in) :: js !< Start j-index
3919 integer, intent(in) :: je !< End j-index
3920 logical, optional, intent(in) :: flag !< Flag to indicate to allocate
3921
3922121 if (present(flag)) then ; if (flag) then ; if (.not.associated(array)) then
39232844850 allocate(array(is:ie,js:je), source=0.0)
3924 endif ; endif ; endif

39270subroutine myAlloc_3d(array, is, ie, js, je, ks, ke, flag)
3928 real, dimension(:,:,:), pointer :: array !< Array to be allocated
3929 integer, intent(in) :: is !< Start i-index
3930 integer, intent(in) :: ie !< End i-index
3931 integer, intent(in) :: js !< Start j-index
3932 integer, intent(in) :: je !< End j-index
3933 integer, intent(in) :: ks !< Start k-index
3934 integer, intent(in) :: ke !< End k-index
3935 logical, optional, intent(in) :: flag !< Flag to indicate to allocate
3936
39370 if (present(flag)) then ; if (flag) then ; if (.not.associated(array)) then
39380 allocate(array(is:ie,js:je,ks:ke), source=0.0)
3939 endif ; endif ; endif

4043!< Rotate the fluxes by a set number of quarter turns
40440subroutine rotate_forcing(fluxes_in, fluxes, turns)
4045 type(forcing), intent(in) :: fluxes_in !< Input forcing structure
4046 type(forcing), intent(inout) :: fluxes !< Rotated forcing structure
4047 integer, intent(in) :: turns !< Number of quarter turns
4048
4049 logical :: do_ustar, do_taumag, do_water, do_heat, do_salt, do_press, do_shelf, &
4050 do_iceberg, do_heat_added, do_buoy
4051
4052 call get_forcing_groups(fluxes_in, do_water, do_heat, do_ustar, do_taumag, do_press, &
40530 do_shelf, do_iceberg, do_salt, do_heat_added, do_buoy)
4054
40550 if (associated(fluxes_in%ustar)) &
40560 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%ustar, turns, fluxes%ustar)
40570 if (associated(fluxes_in%ustar_gustless)) &
40580 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%ustar_gustless, turns, fluxes%ustar_gustless)
4059
40600 if (associated(fluxes_in%tau_mag)) &
40610 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%tau_mag, turns, fluxes%tau_mag)
40620 if (associated(fluxes_in%tau_mag_gustless)) &
40630 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%tau_mag_gustless, turns, fluxes%tau_mag_gustless)
4064
40650 if (do_water) then
40660 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%evap, turns, fluxes%evap)
40670 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%lprec, turns, fluxes%lprec)
40680 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%fprec, turns, fluxes%fprec)
40690 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%vprec, turns, fluxes%vprec)
40700 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%lrunoff, turns, fluxes%lrunoff)
40710 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%frunoff, turns, fluxes%frunoff)
40720 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%lrunoff_glc, turns, fluxes%lrunoff_glc)
40730 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%frunoff_glc, turns, fluxes%frunoff_glc)
40740 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%seaice_melt, turns, fluxes%seaice_melt)
40750 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%netMassOut, turns, fluxes%netMassOut)
40760 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%netMassIn, turns, fluxes%netMassIn)
4077 endif
4078
40790 if (do_heat) then
40800 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%seaice_melt_heat, turns, fluxes%seaice_melt_heat)
40810 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%sw, turns, fluxes%sw)
40820 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%lw, turns, fluxes%lw)
40830 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%latent, turns, fluxes%latent)
40840 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%sens, turns, fluxes%sens)
40850 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%latent_evap_diag, turns, fluxes%latent_evap_diag)
40860 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%latent_fprec_diag, turns, fluxes%latent_fprec_diag)
40870 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%latent_frunoff_diag, turns, fluxes%latent_frunoff_diag)
40880 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%latent_frunoff_glc_diag, turns, fluxes%latent_frunoff_glc_diag)
4089 endif
4090
40910 if (do_salt) then
40920 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%salt_flux, turns, fluxes%salt_flux)
4093 endif
4094
40950 if (do_heat .and. do_water) then
40960 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_content_cond, turns, fluxes%heat_content_cond)
40970 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_content_lprec, turns, fluxes%heat_content_lprec)
40980 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_content_fprec, turns, fluxes%heat_content_fprec)
40990 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_content_vprec, turns, fluxes%heat_content_vprec)
41000 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_content_lrunoff, turns, fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff)
41010 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_content_lrunoff_glc, turns, fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc)
41020 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_content_frunoff, turns, fluxes%heat_content_frunoff)
41030 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_content_frunoff_glc, turns, fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc)
41040 if (associated (fluxes_in%heat_content_evap)) then
41050 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_content_evap, turns, fluxes%heat_content_evap)
4106 else
41070 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_content_massout, turns, fluxes%heat_content_massout)
41080 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_content_massin, turns, fluxes%heat_content_massin)
4109 endif
4110 endif
4111
41120 if (do_press) then
41130 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%p_surf, turns, fluxes%p_surf)
4114 endif
4115
41160 if (do_shelf) then
41170 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%frac_shelf_h, turns, fluxes%frac_shelf_h)
41180 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%ustar_shelf, turns, fluxes%ustar_shelf)
41190 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%iceshelf_melt, turns, fluxes%iceshelf_melt)
41200 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%shelf_sfc_mass_flux, turns, fluxes%shelf_sfc_mass_flux)
4121 endif
4122
41230 if (do_iceberg) then
41240 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%ustar_berg, turns, fluxes%ustar_berg)
41250 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%area_berg, turns, fluxes%area_berg)
4126 endif
4127
41280 if (do_heat_added) then
41290 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%heat_added, turns, fluxes%heat_added)
4130 endif
4131
4132 ! The following fields are handled by drivers rather than control flags.
41330 if (associated(fluxes_in%sw_vis_dir)) &
41340 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%sw_vis_dir, turns, fluxes%sw_vis_dir)
41350 if (associated(fluxes_in%sw_vis_dif)) &
41360 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%sw_vis_dif, turns, fluxes%sw_vis_dif)
41370 if (associated(fluxes_in%sw_nir_dir)) &
41380 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%sw_nir_dir, turns, fluxes%sw_nir_dir)
41390 if (associated(fluxes_in%sw_nir_dif)) &
41400 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%sw_nir_dif, turns, fluxes%sw_nir_dif)
4141
41420 if (associated(fluxes_in%salt_flux_in)) &
41430 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%salt_flux_in, turns, fluxes%salt_flux_in)
41440 if (associated(fluxes_in%salt_flux_added)) &
41450 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%salt_flux_added, turns, fluxes%salt_flux_added)
4146
41470 if (associated(fluxes_in%p_surf_full)) &
41480 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%p_surf_full, turns, fluxes%p_surf_full)
4149
41500 if (associated(fluxes_in%buoy)) &
41510 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%buoy, turns, fluxes%buoy)
4152
41530 if (associated(fluxes_in%BBL_tidal_dis)) &
41540 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%BBL_tidal_dis, turns, fluxes%BBL_tidal_dis)
41550 if (associated(fluxes_in%ustar_tidal)) &
41560 call rotate_array(fluxes_in%ustar_tidal, turns, fluxes%ustar_tidal)
4157
4158 ! NOTE: Tracer fields are handled by FMS, so are left unrotated. Any
4159 ! reads/writes to tr_fields must be appropriately rotated.
41600 if (coupler_type_initialized(fluxes%tr_fluxes)) then
41610 call coupler_type_copy_data(fluxes_in%tr_fluxes, fluxes%tr_fluxes)
4162 endif
4163
4164 ! Scalars and flags
41650 fluxes%accumulate_p_surf = fluxes_in%accumulate_p_surf
4166
41670 fluxes%vPrecGlobalAdj = fluxes_in%vPrecGlobalAdj
41680 fluxes%saltFluxGlobalAdj = fluxes_in%saltFluxGlobalAdj
41690 fluxes%netFWGlobalAdj = fluxes_in%netFWGlobalAdj
41700 fluxes%vPrecGlobalScl = fluxes_in%vPrecGlobalScl
41710 fluxes%saltFluxGlobalScl = fluxes_in%saltFluxGlobalScl
41720 fluxes%netFWGlobalScl = fluxes_in%netFWGlobalScl
4173
41740 fluxes%fluxes_used = fluxes_in%fluxes_used
41750 fluxes%dt_buoy_accum = fluxes_in%dt_buoy_accum
41760 fluxes%C_p = fluxes_in%C_p
4177 ! NOTE: gustless_accum_bug is set during allocation
4178
41790 fluxes%num_msg = fluxes_in%num_msg
41800 fluxes%max_msg = fluxes_in%max_msg
41810end subroutine rotate_forcing

4183!< Rotate the forcing fields from the input domain
41840subroutine rotate_mech_forcing(forces_in, turns, forces)
4185 type(mech_forcing), intent(in) :: forces_in !< Forcing on the input domain
4186 integer, intent(in) :: turns !< Number of quarter-turns
4187 type(mech_forcing), intent(inout) :: forces !< Forcing on the rotated domain
4188
4189 logical :: do_stress, do_ustar, do_tau_mag, do_shelf, do_press, do_iceberg
4190
4191 call get_mech_forcing_groups(forces_in, do_stress, do_ustar, do_tau_mag, do_shelf, &
41920 do_press, do_iceberg)
4193
41940 if (do_stress) &
4195 call rotate_vector(forces_in%taux, forces_in%tauy, turns, &
41960 forces%taux, forces%tauy)
4197
41980 if (associated(forces_in%ustar)) &
41990 call rotate_array(forces_in%ustar, turns, forces%ustar)
42000 if (associated(forces_in%tau_mag)) &
42010 call rotate_array(forces_in%tau_mag, turns, forces%tau_mag)
4202
42030 if (do_shelf) then
4204 call rotate_array_pair( &
4205 forces_in%rigidity_ice_u, forces_in%rigidity_ice_v, turns, &
4206 forces%rigidity_ice_u, forces%rigidity_ice_v &
42070 )
4208 call rotate_array_pair( &
4209 forces_in%frac_shelf_u, forces_in%frac_shelf_v, turns, &
4210 forces%frac_shelf_u, forces%frac_shelf_v &
42110 )
4212 endif
4213
42140 if (do_press) then
42150 call rotate_array(forces_in%p_surf, turns, forces%p_surf)
42160 call rotate_array(forces_in%p_surf_full, turns, forces%p_surf_full)
42170 call rotate_array(forces_in%net_mass_src, turns, forces%net_mass_src)
4218
4219 ! p_surf_SSH points to either p_surf or p_surf_full
42200 if (associated(forces_in%p_surf_SSH, forces_in%p_surf)) then
42210 forces%p_surf_SSH => forces%p_surf
42220 else if (associated(forces_in%p_surf_SSH, forces_in%p_surf_full)) then
42230 forces%p_surf_SSH => forces%p_surf_full
4224 else
42250 forces%p_surf_SSH => null()
4226 endif
4227 endif
4228
42290 if (do_iceberg) then
42300 call rotate_array(forces_in%area_berg, turns, forces%area_berg)
42310 call rotate_array(forces_in%mass_berg, turns, forces%mass_berg)
4232 endif
4233
4234 ! Copy fields
42350 forces%dt_force_accum = forces_in%dt_force_accum
42360 forces%net_mass_src_set = forces_in%net_mass_src_set
42370 forces%accumulate_p_surf = forces_in%accumulate_p_surf
42380 forces%accumulate_rigidity = forces_in%accumulate_rigidity
42390 forces%initialized = forces_in%initialized
42400end subroutine rotate_mech_forcing

4242!< Homogenize the forcing fields from the input domain
42430subroutine homogenize_mech_forcing(forces, G, US, Rho0, UpdateUstar)
4244 type(mech_forcing), intent(inout) :: forces !< Forcing on the input domain
4245 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Grid metric of target forcing
4246 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
4247 real, intent(in) :: Rho0 !< A reference density of seawater [R ~> kg m-3],
4248 !! as used to calculate ustar.
4249 logical, optional, intent(in) :: UpdateUstar !< A logical to determine if Ustar should be directly averaged
4250 !! or updated from mean tau.
4251
4252 real :: tx_mean, ty_mean ! Mean wind stresses [R L Z T-2 ~> Pa]
4253 real :: tau_mag ! The magnitude of the wind stresses [R Z2 T-2 ~> Pa]
4254 real :: Irho0 ! Inverse of the mean density [R-1 ~> m3 kg-1]
4255 logical :: do_stress, do_ustar, do_taumag, do_shelf, do_press, do_iceberg, tau2ustar
4256 integer :: i, j, is, ie, js, je, isB, ieB, jsB, jeB
42570 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec
42580 isB = G%iscB ; ieB = G%iecB ; jsB = G%jscB ; jeB = G%jecB
4259
42600 Irho0 = 1.0 / Rho0
4261
4262 call get_mech_forcing_groups(forces, do_stress, do_ustar, do_taumag, do_shelf, &
42630 do_press, do_iceberg)
4264
42650 if (do_stress) then
42660 tx_mean = global_area_mean_u(forces%taux, G, tmp_scale=US%RLZ_T2_to_Pa)
42670 do j=js,je ; do i=isB,ieB
42680 if (G%mask2dCu(I,j) > 0.0) forces%taux(I,j) = tx_mean
4269 enddo ; enddo
42700 ty_mean = global_area_mean_v(forces%tauy, G, tmp_scale=US%RLZ_T2_to_Pa)
42710 do j=jsB,jeB ; do i=is,ie
42720 if (G%mask2dCv(i,J) > 0.0) forces%tauy(i,J) = ty_mean
4273 enddo ; enddo
42740 tau2ustar = .false. ; if (present(UpdateUstar)) tau2ustar = UpdateUstar
42750 if (tau2ustar) then
42760 tau_mag = US%L_to_Z*sqrt((tx_mean**2) + (ty_mean**2))
42770 if (associated(forces%tau_mag)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie ; if (G%mask2dT(i,j) > 0.0) then
42780 forces%tau_mag(i,j) = tau_mag
4279 endif ; enddo ; enddo ; endif
42800 if (associated(forces%ustar)) then ; do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie ; if (G%mask2dT(i,j) > 0.0) then
42810 forces%ustar(i,j) = sqrt(tau_mag * Irho0)
4282 endif ; enddo ; enddo ; endif
4283 else
42840 if (associated(forces%ustar)) &
42850 call homogenize_field_t(forces%ustar, G, tmp_scale=US%Z_to_m*US%s_to_T)
42860 if (associated(forces%tau_mag)) &
42870 call homogenize_field_t(forces%tau_mag, G, tmp_scale=US%RLZ_T2_to_Pa*US%Z_to_L)
4288 endif
4289 else
42900 if (associated(forces%ustar)) &
42910 call homogenize_field_t(forces%ustar, G, tmp_scale=US%Z_to_m*US%s_to_T)
42920 if (associated(forces%tau_mag)) &
42930 call homogenize_field_t(forces%tau_mag, G, tmp_scale=US%RLZ_T2_to_Pa*US%Z_to_L)
4294 endif
4295
42960 if (do_shelf) then
42970 call homogenize_field_u(forces%rigidity_ice_u, G, tmp_scale=US%L_T_to_m_s*US%L_to_m**2*US%L_to_Z)
42980 call homogenize_field_v(forces%rigidity_ice_v, G, tmp_scale=US%L_T_to_m_s*US%L_to_m**2*US%L_to_Z)
42990 call homogenize_field_u(forces%frac_shelf_u, G)
43000 call homogenize_field_v(forces%frac_shelf_v, G)
4301 endif
4302
43030 if (do_press) then
4304 ! NOTE: p_surf_SSH either points to p_surf or p_surf_full
43050 call homogenize_field_t(forces%p_surf, G, tmp_scale=US%RL2_T2_to_Pa)
43060 call homogenize_field_t(forces%p_surf_full, G, tmp_scale=US%RL2_T2_to_Pa)
43070 call homogenize_field_t(forces%net_mass_src, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
4308 endif
4309
43100 if (do_iceberg) then
43110 call homogenize_field_t(forces%area_berg, G)
43120 call homogenize_field_t(forces%mass_berg, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_to_kg_m2)
4313 endif
4314
43150end subroutine homogenize_mech_forcing
4316
4317!< Homogenize the fluxes
43180subroutine homogenize_forcing(fluxes, G, GV, US)
4319 type(forcing), intent(inout) :: fluxes !< Input forcing struct
4320 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Grid metric of target forcing
4321 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< ocean vertical grid structure
4322 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
4323
4324 logical :: do_ustar, do_taumag, do_water, do_heat, do_salt, do_press, do_shelf
4325 logical :: do_iceberg, do_heat_added, do_buoy
4326
4327 call get_forcing_groups(fluxes, do_water, do_heat, do_ustar, do_taumag, do_press, &
43280 do_shelf, do_iceberg, do_salt, do_heat_added, do_buoy)
4329
43300 if (associated(fluxes%ustar)) &
43310 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%ustar, G, tmp_scale=US%Z_to_m*US%s_to_T)
43320 if (associated(fluxes%ustar_gustless)) &
43330 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%ustar_gustless, G, tmp_scale=US%Z_to_m*US%s_to_T)
4334
43350 if (associated(fluxes%tau_mag)) &
43360 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%tau_mag, G, tmp_scale=US%RLZ_T2_to_Pa*US%Z_to_L)
43370 if (associated(fluxes%tau_mag_gustless)) &
43380 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%tau_mag_gustless, G, tmp_scale=US%RLZ_T2_to_Pa*US%Z_to_L)
4339
43400 if (do_water) then
43410 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%evap, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
43420 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%lprec, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
43430 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%fprec, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
43440 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%vprec, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
43450 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%lrunoff, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
43460 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%frunoff, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
43470 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%lrunoff_glc, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
43480 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%frunoff_glc, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
43490 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%seaice_melt, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
4350 ! These two calls might not be needed.
43510 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%netMassOut, G, tmp_scale=GV%H_to_mks)
43520 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%netMassIn, G, tmp_scale=GV%H_to_mks)
4353 !This was removed and I don't think replaced. Not needed?
4354 !call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%netSalt, G)
4355 endif
4356
43570 if (do_heat) then
43580 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%seaice_melt_heat, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43590 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%sw, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43600 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%lw, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43610 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%latent, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43620 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%sens, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
4363 !### These are for diagnostics only and may not be needed.
43640 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%latent_evap_diag, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43650 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%latent_fprec_diag, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43660 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%latent_frunoff_diag, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43670 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%latent_frunoff_glc_diag, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
4368 endif
4369
43700 if (do_salt) call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%salt_flux, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
4371
43720 if (do_heat .and. do_water) then
43730 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%heat_content_cond, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43740 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%heat_content_lprec, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43750 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%heat_content_fprec, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43760 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%heat_content_vprec, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43770 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43780 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%heat_content_frunoff, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43790 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%heat_content_lrunoff_glc, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43800 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%heat_content_frunoff_glc, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43810 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%heat_content_massout, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
43820 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%heat_content_massin, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
4383 endif
4384
43850 if (do_press) call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%p_surf, G, tmp_scale=US%RL2_T2_to_Pa)
4386
43870 if (do_shelf) then
43880 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%frac_shelf_h, G)
43890 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%ustar_shelf, G, tmp_scale=US%Z_to_m*US%s_to_T)
43900 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%iceshelf_melt, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
43910 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%shelf_sfc_mass_flux, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
4392 endif
4393
43940 if (do_iceberg) then
43950 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%ustar_berg, G, tmp_scale=US%Z_to_m*US%s_to_T)
43960 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%area_berg, G)
4397 endif
4398
43990 if (do_heat_added) then
44000 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%heat_added, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
4401 endif
4402
4403 ! The following fields are handled by drivers rather than control flags.
44040 if (associated(fluxes%sw_vis_dir)) &
44050 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%sw_vis_dir, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
4406
44070 if (associated(fluxes%sw_vis_dif)) &
44080 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%sw_vis_dif, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
4409
44100 if (associated(fluxes%sw_nir_dir)) &
44110 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%sw_nir_dir, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
4412
44130 if (associated(fluxes%sw_nir_dif)) &
44140 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%sw_nir_dif, G, tmp_scale=US%QRZ_T_to_W_m2)
4415
44160 if (associated(fluxes%salt_flux_in)) &
44170 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%salt_flux_in, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
4418
44190 if (associated(fluxes%salt_flux_added)) &
44200 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%salt_flux_added, G, tmp_scale=US%RZ_T_to_kg_m2s)
4421
44220 if (associated(fluxes%p_surf_full)) &
44230 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%p_surf_full, G, tmp_scale=US%RL2_T2_to_Pa)
4424
44250 if (associated(fluxes%buoy)) &
44260 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%buoy, G, tmp_scale=US%L_to_m**2*US%s_to_T**3)
4427
44280 if (associated(fluxes%BBL_tidal_dis)) &
44290 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%BBL_tidal_dis, G, tmp_scale=US%L_to_Z**2*US%RZ3_T3_to_W_m2)
4430
44310 if (associated(fluxes%ustar_tidal)) &
44320 call homogenize_field_t(fluxes%ustar_tidal, G, tmp_scale=US%Z_to_m*US%s_to_T)
4433
4434 ! TODO: tracer flux homogenization
4435 ! Having a warning causes a lot of errors (each time step).
4436 !if (coupler_type_initialized(fluxes%tr_fluxes)) &
4437 ! call MOM_error(WARNING, "Homogenization of tracer BC fluxes not yet implemented.")
4438
44390end subroutine homogenize_forcing

44410subroutine homogenize_field_t(var, G, tmp_scale)
4442 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure
4443 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(inout) :: var !< The variable to homogenize [A ~> a]
4444 real, optional, intent(in) :: tmp_scale !< A temporary rescaling factor for the
4445 !! variable that is reversed in the
4446 !! return value [a A-1 ~> 1]
4447
4448 real :: avg ! Global average of var, in the same units as var [A ~> a]
4449 integer :: i, j, is, ie, js, je
44500 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec
4451
44520 avg = global_area_mean(var, G, tmp_scale=tmp_scale)
44530 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
44540 if (G%mask2dT(i,j) > 0.0) var(i,j) = avg
4455 enddo ; enddo
4456
44570end subroutine homogenize_field_t
4458
44590subroutine homogenize_field_v(var, G, tmp_scale)
4460 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure
4461 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G)), intent(inout) :: var !< The variable to homogenize [A ~> a]
4462 real, optional, intent(in) :: tmp_scale !< A temporary rescaling factor for the
4463 !! variable that is reversed in the
4464 !! return value [a A-1 ~> 1]
4465
4466 real :: avg ! Global average of var, in the same units as var [A ~> a]
4467 integer :: i, j, is, ie, jsB, jeB
44680 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; jsB = G%jscB ; jeB = G%jecB
4469
44700 avg = global_area_mean_v(var, G, tmp_scale=tmp_scale)
44710 do J=jsB,jeB ; do i=is,ie
44720 if (G%mask2dCv(i,J) > 0.0) var(i,J) = avg
4473 enddo ; enddo
4474
44750end subroutine homogenize_field_v
4476
44770subroutine homogenize_field_u(var, G, tmp_scale)
4478 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure
4479 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G)), intent(inout) :: var !< The variable to homogenize [A ~> a]
4480 real, optional, intent(in) :: tmp_scale !< A temporary rescaling factor for the
4481 !! variable that is reversed in the
4482 !! return value [a A-1 ~> 1]
4483
4484 real :: avg ! Global average of var, in the same units as var [A ~> a]
4485 integer :: i, j, isB, ieB, js, je
44860 isB = G%iscB ; ieB = G%iecB ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec
4487
44880 avg = global_area_mean_u(var, G, tmp_scale=tmp_scale)
44890 do j=js,je ; do I=isB,ieB
44900 if (G%mask2dCu(I,j) > 0.0) var(I,j) = avg
4491 enddo ; enddo
4492
44930end subroutine homogenize_field_u
4494
4495!> \namespace mom_forcing_type
4496!!
4497!! \section section_fluxes Boundary fluxes
4498!!
4499!! The ocean is a forced-dissipative system. Forcing occurs at the
4500!! boundaries, and this module mediates the various forcing terms
4501!! from momentum, heat, salt, and mass. Boundary fluxes from other
4502!! tracers are treated by coupling to biogeochemical models. We
4503!! here present elements of how MOM6 assumes boundary fluxes are
4504!! passed into the ocean.
4505!!
4506!! Note that all fluxes are positive into the ocean. For surface
4507!! boundary fluxes, that means fluxes are positive downward.
4508!! For example, a positive shortwave flux warms the ocean.
4509!!
4510!! \subsection subsection_momentum_fluxes Surface boundary momentum fluxes
4511!!
4512!! The ocean surface exchanges momentum with the overlying atmosphere,
4513!! sea ice, and land ice. The momentum is exchanged as a horizontal
4514!! stress (Newtons per squared meter: N/m2) imposed on the upper ocean
4515!! grid cell.
4516!!
4517!! \subsection subsection_mass_fluxes Surface boundary mass fluxes
4518!!
4519!! The ocean gains or loses mass through evaporation, precipitation,
4520!! sea ice melt/form, and river runoff. Positive mass fluxes
4521!! add mass to the liquid ocean. The boundary mass flux units are
4522!! (kilogram per square meter per sec: kg/(m2/sec)).
4523!!
4524!! * Evaporation field can in fact represent a
4525!! mass loss (evaporation) or mass gain (condensation in foggy areas).
4526!! * sea ice formation leads to mass moving from the liquid ocean to the
4527!! ice model, and melt adds liquid to the ocean.
4528!! * Precipitation can be liquid or frozen (snow). Furthermore, in
4529!! some versions of the GFDL coupler, precipitation can be negative.
4530!! The reason is that the ice model combines precipitation with
4531!! ice melt and ice formation. This limitation of the ice model
4532!! diagnostics should be overcome future versions.
4533!! * River runoff can be liquid or frozen. Frozen runoff is often
4534!! associated with calving land-ice and/or ice bergs.
4535!!
4536!! \subsection subsection_salt_fluxes Surface boundary salt fluxes
4537!!
4538!! Over most of the ocean, there is no exchange of salt with the
4539!! atmosphere. However, the liquid ocean exchanges salt with sea ice.
4540!! When ice forms, it extracts salt from ice pockets and discharges the
4541!! salt into the liquid ocean. The salt concentration of sea ice
4542!! is therefore much lower (around 5ppt) than liquid seawater
4543!! (around 30-35ppt in high latitudes).
4544!!
4545!! For ocean-ice models run with a prescribed atmosphere, such as
4546!! in the CORE/OMMIP simulations, it is necessary to employ a surface
4547!! restoring term to the k=1 salinity equation, thus imposing a salt
4548!! flux onto the ocean even outside of sea ice regimes. This salt
4549!! flux is non-physical, and represents a limitation of the ocean-ice
4550!! models run without an interactive atmosphere. Sometimes this salt
4551!! flux is converted to an implied fresh water flux. However, doing
4552!! so generally leads to changes in the sea level, unless a global
4553!! normalization is provided to zero-out the net water flux.
4554!! As a complement, for models with a restoring salt flux, one may
4555!! choose to zero-out the net salt entering the ocean. There are
4556!! pros/cons of each approach.
4557!!
4558!!
4559!! \subsection subsection_heat_fluxes Surface boundary heat fluxes
4560!!
4561!! There are many terms that contribute to boundary-related heating
4562!! of the k=1 surface model grid cell. We here outline details of
4563!! this heat, with each term having units W/m2.
4564!!
4565!! The net flux of heat crossing ocean surface is stored in the diagnostic
4566!! array "hfds". This array is computed as
4567!! \f[
4568!! \mbox{hfds = shortwave + longwave + latent + sensible + mass transfer + frazil + restore + flux adjustments}
4569!! \f]
4570!!
4571!! * shortwave (SW) = shortwave radiation (always warms ocean)
4572!! * longwave (LW) = longwave radiation (generally cools ocean)
4573!! * latent (LAT) = turbulent latent heat loss due to evaporation
4574!! (liquid to vapor) or melt (snow to liquid); generally
4575!! cools the ocean
4576!! * sensible (SENS) = turbulent heat transfer due to differences in
4577!! air-sea or ice-sea temperature
4578!! * mass transfer (MASS) = heat transfer due to heat content of mass (e.g., E-P+R)
4579!! transferred across ocean surface; computed relative
4580!! to 0 Celsius
4581!! * frazil (FRAZ) = heat transferred to form frazil sea ice
4582!! (positive heating of liquid ocean)
4583!! * restore (RES) = heat from surface damping sometimes imposed
4584!! in non-coupled model simulations .
4585!! * restore (flux adjustments) = heat from surface flux adjustment.
4586!!
4587!! \subsubsection subsubsection_SW Treatment of shortwave
4588!!
4589!! The shortwave field itself is split into two pieces:
4590!!
4591!! * shortwave = penetrative SW + non-penetrative SW
4592!! * non-penetrative = non-downwelling shortwave; portion of SW
4593!! totally absorbed in the k=1 cell.
4594!! The non-penetrative SW is combined with
4595!! LW+LAT+SENS+seaice_melt_heat in net_heat inside routine
4596!! extractFluxes1d. Notably, for many cases,
4597!! non-penetrative SW = 0.
4598!! * penetrative = that portion of shortwave penetrating below
4599!! a tiny surface layer. This is the downwelling
4600!! shortwave. Penetrative SW participates in
4601!! the penetrative SW heating of k=1,nz cells,
4602!! with the amount of penetration dependent on
4603!! optical properties.
4604!!
4605!! \subsubsection subsubsection_bdy_heating Convergence of heat into the k=1 cell
4606!!
4607!! The convergence of boundary-related heat into surface grid cell is
4608!! given by the difference in the net heat entering the top of the k=1
4609!! cell and the penetrative SW leaving the bottom of the cell.
4610!! \f{eqnarray*}
4611!! Q(k=1) &=& \mbox{hfds} - \mbox{pen}\_\mbox{SW(leaving bottom of k=1)}
4612!! \\ &=& \mbox{nonpen}\_\mbox{SW} + (\mbox{pen}\_\mbox{SW(enter k=1)}-\mbox{pen}\_\mbox{SW(leave k=1)})
4613!! + \mbox{LW+LAT+SENS+MASS+FRAZ+RES}
4614!! \\ &=& \mbox{nonpen}\_\mbox{SW}+ \mbox{LW+LAT+SENS+MASS+FRAZ+RES}
4615!! + [\mbox{pen}\_\mbox{SW(enter k=1)} - \mbox{pen}\_\mbox{SW(leave k=1)}]
4616!! \f}
4617!! The convergence of the penetrative shortwave flux is given by
4618!! \f$ \mbox{pen}\_\mbox{SW (enter k)}-\mbox{pen}\_\mbox{SW (leave k)}\f$. This term
4619!! appears for all cells k=1,nz. It is diagnosed as "rsdoabsorb" inside module
4620!! MOM6/src/parameterizations/vertical/MOM_diabatic_aux.F90
4621!!
4622
46230end module MOM_forcing_type
